kaṟpiṉkaṇṇē poruḷviṉi kuṟittuc cēṇiṭip pirintu mīṇṭu pōtarum talimakaṉi vilakkum urimi tōḻikku illiyāyiṉum, iruḷkūr iravuppōtiṉ kaṇ iṭinilattut taṅkātu vantami paṟṟi ētam āyntu avaṟku muṉṉilik kiḷaviyāṟ kūṟukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, talimakaṉ varum vaḻiyiṉ ētaṅkaḷik kuṟippāḷāy, koliviṉiyarākiya āṟalippōr mikka vaḻi eṉpāḷ, patukkit tāya otuk karuṅ kavali eṉṟum, iravil kāṭṭil tiriyum yāṉi makkaṭkup perumpaki yākalāṉ, yāṉi uṟupaki niṉi yātu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ; “iraviṉ varalvēṇṭā, yāṉi yuṭiya curam 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. niṉintavaḻi ettiṟat tōrum ūkkam kuṉṟuvar eṉpāḷ niṉiyātu eṉṟum, niṉṉip pirintuṟiyum talimakaḷ eytiya melivuṇarntu niṟinta kātal celuttak kaṭuki varukiṉṟāy eṉpāḷ, aruḷ puri neñcam turappa eṉṟum, neṟiyaṟiyāvaki maṟittaliṉ iravuk kālatti iruḷpora niṉṟa iravu eṉṟum, neṟiyiṉ koṭumi yum iṭiyil uḷavākum ētamum karuti, niṉ pēraruḷukku añcukiṉṟēm eṉpāḷ, yāṅku vantaṉiyō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
kaṟpiṉkaṇ ivvāṟu orōvaḻit talimakaṉpāl mikka kāmattu miṭaluṭic ceyal tōṉṟiṉ, atu, kaḻikāmamāyt tīṅku viḷiyāmik kuṟikkoṇṭu kūṟaliṉ, tōḻikku im muṉṉilik kiḷavi amivatāyiṟṟu. “uṟukaṇ ōmpal taṉ ṉiyal pākaliṉ, uriya tākum tōḻikaṇ uraṉē 2” eṉa āciriyar urippatu kāṇka. kaḻikāmam tīṅku payattali, “kāmam vekuḷi mayakkam ivimūṉṟaṉ, nāmam keṭakkeṭum nōy 3” eṉṟum, “ciṉamē kāmam kaḻikaṇ ṇōṭṭam, accam poyccol aṉpumika vuṭimi, teṟalkaṭu miyoṭu piṟavum ivvulakattu, aṟanteri tikirikku vaḻiyaṭi yākum 4” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr teruṭṭumāṟu kāṇka.
“peṟaṟkarum perumporuḷ 1” eṉṟa cūttirattup “piriyuṅ kāli etirniṉṟu cāṟṟiya, marapuṭi etirum uḷappaṭap  piṟavum” eṉpuḻip piṟavum eṉṟataṉāl itaṉi amittu,  itu cēṇiṭip pirintu iraviṉ vantuḻik kūṟiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
	363.	ciliviṟ pakaḻic centuva rāṭik
kolivi leyiṉar taṅkiniṉ muliya
cuṇaṅkeṉa niṉiti nīyē
aṇaṅkeṉa niṉiyumeṉ ṉaṇaṅkuṟu neñcē.
itu, puṇarntuṭaṉ celkiṉṟa talimakaṉ iṭiccurattut talimakaḷi nalam pārāṭṭiyatu.
uri :
cilimarattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa villiṭit toṭukkum ampiṉiyum, civanta āṭiyiṉiyum, kolit toḻiṟ kuriya villiṉiyu muṭiya vēṭṭuvarkkut taṅkiyē,  niṉ mulikkaṇṇavākiya tēmali nī cuṇaṅkeṉa niṉik kiṉṟaṉi; maṟṟu, nōy mikka eṉ neñcam ataṉi varuttam tarum aṇaṅku eṉa niṉiyāniṉṟatukāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
cilivil ciliyeṉṉum marattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vil; “arimāṉ iṭittaṉṉa añcili valvil2” eṉṟum, “cili māṇ valvil3” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr urippatu kāṇka. cilittal, olittalumām. kolivil eṉṟaviṭattu uṭiyavaṉ toḻil viṉmēl ēṟṟappaṭṭatu. centuvarāṭi yeṉṟatu, cemmiyākiya niṟappaṇpiṉ mikuti yuṇara niṉṟatu. “tuvarcey āṭic centoṭi maṟavar 4” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟiṉar.
cuṇaṅku niṉakku aḻaku taruvatāyiṉum eṉakku varuttam taruvatāyiṟṟu eṉpatām. aṇaṅku taruvataṉi aṇaṅkeṉ ṟāṉ. “aṇaṅkeṉa urutta cuṇaṅkaṇi ākattu, īrkkiṭi pōkā vēriḷa vaṉamuli 5” eṉṟār piṟarum. mulikku nalam cuṇaṅkoṭu tikaḻta lākaliṉ, aṇaṅkeṉa niṉiyum eṉṟatu nalam pārāṭṭiyavāṟu.
iḵtu, uṭaṉpōkiṉṟāṉ, perunāṇattāl varuntum talimakaḷatu melivu nīkkum karuttāl muṉṉilippaṭuttit  taṉ perunayappāl teḷivittavāṟu eṉak koḷka.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkiṉṟāṉ nalam pārāṭṭiya kūṟṟām eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 1. “centuvarāṭi” eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, kolivil eyiṉar taṅki yeṉap puṇartaṟ poruṇmi vantatāyiṉum pālikkuriya makkaṭpeyar kūṟutaliṉ pāliyāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar 2. 
	364.	muḷamā valci yeyiṉar taṅki
iḷamā veyiṟṟikku niṉṉili yaṟiyac
colliṉe ṉirakku maḷavi
veḷvēl viṭali viriyā tīmē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkku nayanta talimakaṉ ataṉit tōḻikku uṇartta, avaḷ muṭippāḷāyc colliyatu.
uri :
veḷḷiya vēliyuṭiya taliva, muḷḷiyuṭiya māvākiya paṉṟiyiṉi uṇavāka vuṭiya vēṭṭuvarkkut taṅkiyākiya iḷamiyum aḻaku muṭiya eyiṟṟikku, niṉ uṭaṉpōkkuk kuṟippi viḷaṅka urittu, uṭaṉpaṭumāṟu irantukoṇṭu yāṉ varum aḷavum, viriyātu niṟpāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
muḷamā, muḷḷiyuṭiya māvākiya paṉṟi; itu muḷavumā eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. “kāṉava ṉeyta muḷavumāṉ koḻuṅkuṟi3” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa. īṇṭu niṉ nili eṉpatu, uṭaṉpōkku nayantuḷḷa niṉ maṉaniliyi eṉṟavāṟu: colliṉeṉ. muṟṟeccam. irattal, iṇaṅkappeṟutal. viriyātīmē eṉṟatu, virintu ceytaṟkariyatu eṉpatu paṭa niṉṟatu.
veḷippaṭi kuṟippu eṉṟa iruvakiyālum paṉmuṟi varivu kaṭāyavaḻiyum kaḷavē virumpi yoḻukiyavaṉ, tōḻi yiṉ kuṟippiṉṟiyē uṭaṉpōkku nayantu avaḷpāl uṇart tiṉāṉākaliṉ, avaḷ talimakaḷatu kuṟippaṟintu pōkkuṭaṉ paṭutta virikiṉṟamipaṟṟi, iḷamā eyiṟṟikku niṉṉili aṟiyac colliṉeṉ irakku maḷavi eṉṟāḷ. iḷiyaḷā taliṉ, avaṭku aṟiyac colla vēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, aṟiyac colliṉeṉ eṉṟāḷ. itukāṟum kaḷavē virumpi yoḻukiya nī, itu tuṇintatu avaṭku maruṭki viḷivikkumākaliṉ, niṉṉili aṟiyac colla vēṇṭuvatu kaṭaṉ eṉṟumām. “tanti taṉṉiyar uṭaṉiruppac cāṉṟōr cūḻa nikaḻum vatuvi naṉmaṇam 1” karutiyiruntāḷ, ivvuṭaṉpōkkukku icital eḷitiṉ iyaluvataṉṟu eṉpatu tōṉṟa irakkum aḷavi eṉṟum, niṉpāṟ kāṇappaṭum virivu avaḷpāl illiyākaliṉ, yāṉ irakkumaḷavi amitalvēṇṭum eṉpāḷ, viriyātīmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
iṉi uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ neṟiyiṭi uḷavākum ētamum, uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi yuṇṭākum alarum kūṟit talimakaḷ nāṇiyavaḻit takuvaṉa kūṟi uṭaṉpaṭuvikka vēṇṭutaliṉ, irakkumaḷavum eṉṟum, kuṟivaḻiyum oruvaḻit taṇantum oḻukukiṉṟā ṉākaliṉ viriyātīmē eṉṟum tōḻi irantu niṉṟāḷ eṉṟumām.
itu, tōḻipāl tōṉṟavēṇṭiya uṭaṉpōkkiṉit talimakaṉ tāṉē nayantu muṟpaṭakkūṟutaliṉ, tōḻi muṉ ṉilikkiḷaviyāl pōkkuṭaṉpaṭutal kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
itu koṇṭu pōṅkālattuk koṇṭuṭaṉ pōkku oruppaṭuttuval eṉṟatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 2” iḷam pūraṇar, inniliyaṟiya eṉṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, piriyuṅ kālattut talimakaṭku uṇarttukiṉṟēṉ eṉat tōḻi tali makaṟkurittaṟku itaṉik kāṭṭi, itu vilakkiṟṟu eṉpar. 3”
	365.	kaṇamā toliccit taṉṉiyar tanta
niṇavūṉ valcip paṭupuḷ ḷōppum
nalamā ṇeyiṟṟi pōlap palamika
naṉṉalam nayavara vuṭiyi
eṉṉōṟ ṟaṉiyō māviṉ ṟaḷirē.
itu, variviṭivittup pirintu mīḷkiṉṟāṉ, iṭic curattuk kuḻitta māviṉ taḷir kaṇṭu colliyatu.
uri :
kūṭṭamākiya mākkaḷit tolittut taṉṉiyar koṇarnta niṇamākiya taciyuṇavik kavartaṟkup pōta rum paṟavikaḷik kaṭiyum peṇmi nalam ciṟanta eyiṟṟiyip pōla, māviṉ taḷirē, palavāy mikka nalam yāvarum virumpumāṟu uṭiyi; ātaliṉ ataṟku ettavam cey taṉiyō? kūṟuka eṉṟavāṟu.
kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka vāḻum māṉiṉam kaṇamā eṉap paṭṭatu. taṉṉiyar, taṉakku muṟṟōṉṟiyōr; talimi yuṭi yōrumām. ai, talimi. valci, uṇavu. niṇattoṭu kūṭiya ūṉē uṇavātaṟku ēṟṟatākaliṉ, niṇavūṉvalci eṉa vitantu kūṟiṉār. eyiṟṟikku nalamāvatu māmikkaviṉum kaṇṭārāl virumpappaṭum naṟkuṇamuṭimiyumām. naṉ ṉalam eṉṟatu, niṟap polivum meṉmiyum nōkki. naya varavu, viruppam.
eyiṟṟipōla nalampalavum nayavarap peṟutal ariteṉ pāṉ, naṉṉalam nayavara vuṭiyi eṉṟum, ulakattup peṟaṟ kariya poruḷ oṉṟaṉik karutiyavāṟē peṟutal tavamuṭi yārkkuallatu iṉmiyiṉ eṉ nōṟṟaṉiyō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. “vēṇṭiya vēṇṭiyāṅ keytalāl ceytavam, īṇṭu muyalap paṭum 1” eṉpatu kāṇka. makaḷirmēṉi māntaḷir pōṟaliṉ, ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ; “naṟuvaṭip, piṅkaṇ māattu antaḷi raṉṉa, naṉmā mēṉi 2” eṉpataṉālum aṟika. itu, “poruḷē uvamam ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵ tuvama mākum 1” eṉṟataṉāl amiyum.
itu variviṭi vittup pōkiṉṟāṉ māviṉi nōkkik kūṟiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.2 itu muṉṉili yātaṟ killāta māviṉi muṉṉilippaṭuttiṟṟu.
366.	aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉiyeṉ ṟōḻi
pacantaṉaḷ periteṉac civanta kaṇṇi
koṉṉē kaṭavuti yāyi ṉeṉṉatūum
aṟiya vākumō maṟṟē
muṟiyiṇark kōṅkam payanta māṟē.
itu, talimakaḷi nōkki, ivvēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉṟu viṉaviya cevilikkut tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.
paḻiya uri :
itu muṉṉāḷ avaṉ tanta kōṅkampūvāl viḷintatu eṉap pūttarupuṇarcci kūṟiyavā ṟāyiṟṟu. itu karupporuḷāṟ pāli.
uri :
aṉṉāy, vāḻvāyāka. yāṉ kūṟuvataṉi virumpik kēḷ. eṉ tōḻi mikavum pacappeyti vēṟupaṭukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi yeṉa nī ciṉattāl kaṇcivantu accuṟutti viṉavukiṉṟāy. ārāyumiṭattu, atu taḷirum pūṅkottukkaḷu muṭiya kōṅkiṉpūvik koṭut tamiyāl āyiṉamiyiṉ, piṟarāl ettuṇiyum aṟiyap paṭātukāṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
tōḻi eṉṟatu talimakaḷi. kaṇṇi: muṟṟeccam. koṉṉiccol accapporuṭṭu. ēkāram: etirmaṟi. māṟu: ētupporuḷ paṭuvatōr iṭiccol; itaṉi “ataṉiṉiyaṟal 3” eṉṉum cūttirattu “aṉṉa piṟavum” eṉṟa taṉāṟ koḷvar teyvacciliyār. muṟi, taḷir. kōṅkam, pālikkuriya karupporuḷ. mutal, karu, uri eṉṟa poruḷ vaki mūṉṟaṉuḷ, tiṇiyuṇartaṟkaṇ mutaliṟ karuvum karuviṉ uriyum ciṟantaṉa vākaliṉ īṇṭuk karupporuḷ paṟṟi itu pāliyiṟ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “mutalkaru uripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟē, nuvaluṅ kāli muṟiciṟan taṉavē 1” eṉpataṉuḷ, nuvaluṅkāli eṉṟataṉāl iḵtu amiyum eṉa aṟika.
itaṉkaṇ, pacantaṉaḷ peritu eṉṟatu, cevili kūṟiya muṉṉilikkiḷavi. ataṟku aṟattoṭu nilivakiyāl viṭi kūṟukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ aṉṉāy vāḻi vēṇṭu aṉṉi eṉa mukampukuntāḷ. iḷami vaḷarccikkaṇ piṟakkum vaḷami vēṟupāṭṭiṉum, taliviyatu pacappu mikkut tōṉṟutal paṟṟi, eṉtōḻi pacantaṉaḷ peritu eṉṟa cevili kūṟṟik koṇṭeṭuttu moḻintāḷ. piṟāṇṭum, “aṉṉāy vāḻi vēṇ ṭaṉṉi niṉmakaḷ, pālu muṇṇāḷ paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭu, naṉipacan taṉaḷeṉa viṉavuti 2” eṉpatu kāṇka. talimakaḷ eytiya vēṟupāṭṭuk kuriya kāraṇam viḷaṅkāmi yāṉ ciṉaṅkoḷ kiṉṟaṉi eṉpāḷ, civanta kaṇṇi eṉṟum, eṉṉi accuṟuttik kēṭpatiṟ payaṉiṉṟu, “ataṉtiṟam yāṉum teṟṟeṉa uṇarēṉ” eṉpāḷ koṉṉē kaṭavuti eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
aḵtu ārāyntallatu kūṟalākātu eṉṟaṟku āyiṉ eṉṟum, aṟiyāp paruvattē nikaḻntavataṉi aṟiyum paruvamāya ippoḻutu niṉintu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, atu ciṟitum eḷitil aṟiyalākātu eṉpāḷ eṉṉatūum aṟiya vākumō eṉṟum, annikaḻcci iḵtu eṉat tāṉ aṟintavāṟṟāl kūṟuvāṉ toṭaṅki muṟiyiṇark kōṅkam payanta māṟē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itupaṟṟiyē paḻiyavurikārarum, muṉṉāḷ avaṉ tanta kōṅkampūvāl viḷinta pūttaru puṇarcci3 eṉṟār.
	367.	poriyarik kōṅkiṉ poṉmaruḷ pacuvī
viriyiṇar vēṅkiyoṭu vēṟupaṭa milicci
viravumala raṇinta vēṉiṟ kāṉyāṟṟut
tēroṭu kuṟuka vantōṉ
pērōṭu puṇarntaṉ ṟaṉṉiyiva ḷuyirē.
itu, notumalar variviṉkaṇ tōḻi cevilikku aṟat
toṭu niṉṟatu.
paḻiya uri :
kāṉyāṟṟut tēroṭu kuṟuka vantōṉ eṉṟatu, kāṉyāṟṟu yāṅkaḷ aḻuntuḻik kuṟuka vantōṉ eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, puṉaltaru puṇarcci kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
pēroṭu puṇarntaṉṟu ivaḷuyir eṉṟatu, aṉṟu tōṉṟiya iṉṉāṉ tanta uyir eṉa avaṉpēroṭu paṭṭatu ivaḷuyir eṉṟavāṟu. itu kālattāṟ pāli.
uri :
aṉṉāy, poritta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kōṅkiṉ poṉpōlum pūkkaḷ, virinta kottukkaḷiyuṭiya vēṅkip pūvōṭu vēṟupaṭa aṇintu, palavāy viraviya malarkaḷāṟ polinta vēṉiṟparuvattil, kāṉyāṟṟiṉ maruṅkē tērēṟik kuṟuka vantōṉuṭiya peyarōṭu ivaḷuṭiya uyir oṉṟupaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉṟatu, “aṉṟutoṭṭu aṉṉāṉ tanta uyireṉa avaṉ pērōṭupaṭṭatu ivaḷuyir eṉpatām.”
āl, ōmi, koṉṟi, yā, vēṅki, vēmpu mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ iṉattic cērnta tākaliṉ, kōṅkiṉiyum poriyarik kōṅku eṉṟār; “poriyari ālam 1” “yā attup poriyari muḻumutal 2” “poriyari vēmpu 3” “poriyari yōmi 4” “poriyari vēṅki 5” “poriyari ñemirnta puḻaṟkāyk koṉṟi 6” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇka. kōṅkiṉpū poṉpōl iruppatu. “puriniṟa nīḷpoṉ aṇinta kōṅkam 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. ataṉāl “poṉcey kaṉṉam poliya veḷḷi, nuṇkōl  aṟikuṟintu utirvaṉa pōla, arava vaṇṭiṉam ūtutoṟum kuravattu, ōṅkuciṉi naṟuvī kōṅkalar uṟippa 2 ” eṉpārāyiṉar. vēṅkippūvum poṉṉiṟatta tākaliṉ, vēṟupaṭa milicci eṉṟār. viravu
malar, vēṟu vēṟu niṟamuṭiya malar; avi maravam, pātiri mutaliyaṉa. kāṉyāṟu vēṉiṟkālattut teḷintiruppatu paṟṟi vēṉiṟ kāṉyāṟu eṉa vitantu kūṟiṉār. makkaṭku iṭappaṭum peyarellām uyirmēlatākaliṉ, uyiroṭu uṇṭākiya kalappip pēroṭu puṇarntaṉṟu eṉṟār. puṇarntaṉṟu: iṟantakāla muṟṟuviṉi.
kōṅku, vēṅki mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pūkkaḷik koytu māli toṭuttu viḷiyāṭukiyil kāṉyāṟṟu nīr koṇarnta vēṟu pūkkaḷi eṭuttaṟku muyaṉṟapōtu talimakaḷ nīriṭi vīḻntu aḻuntiṉāḷāka, akkālit talimakaṉ vantu uyir tantu utaviṉāṉ eṉpāḷ, viravumalar aṇinta vēṉiṟ kāṉyāṟṟuk kuṟuka vantōṉ eṉṟāḷ. tēroṭu vantāṉ eṉṟāḷ, muṉpu aṟiyāta oru celvattōṉṟal eṉṟaṟku. nīriṉkaṇ vīḻntāḷit tāṉum nīruṭ kuḷittut taṉ mārpiṭip pullik koṇṭu eḻuntāṉ eṉpatu tōṉṟa, kuṟuka eṉṟu kuṟittāḷ. ivvāṟu uyir tantu utaviṉāṟkut taṉṉuyirit tantāḷ eṉpāḷ, vantōṉ pēroṭu puṇarntaṉṟu ivaḷ uyir eṉṟāḷ eṉka. piṟāṇṭum, “kāmar kaṭum puṉal kalan temmōṭu āṭuvāḷ, tāmarikkaṇputittañcit taḷarntu ataṉōṭu oḻukalāṉ, nīṇāka naṟuntaṇṭār tayaṅkap pāyntu aruḷiṉāl, pūṇākam uṟattaḻīip pōntāṉ akaṉakalam, varumuli puṇarntaṉa eṉpataṉāl eṉtōḻi, arumaḻi taralvēṇṭiṉ tarukiṟkum perumiyaḷē 3” eṉṟu urippatu kāṇka. ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē, paḻiyavuri kārarum itu “puṉal taru puṇarcci kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu” eṉṟār. avaṉ tantatu ivaḷuyir; avaṉuṭiyatu ivaḷuyir eṉa uṭiyāṉum uṭimiyumāy iyintoḻintatu eṉṟaṟkup pēroṭu puṇarntaṉṟu ivaḷ uyir eṉṟum, eṉavē, piṟarkku ivaḷatu uyir urittātaṟku iṭamilli eṉṟum urittā ḷāyiṟṟu.
	368.	erippū vilavat tūḻkaḻi paṉmalar
porippūm puṉkiṉ pukarniḻal varikkum
taṇpata vēṉi liṉpa nukarcci
emmoṭu koṇmō perumaniṉ
ammel lōti yaḻivila ḷeṉiṉē.
itu, vēṉiṟkālattu tummoṭu viḷiyāṭṭu nukara varuval eṉṟu paruvaṅkuṟittup piriyaluṟṟa talimakaṟkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
paḻiya uri :
ammel ōti aḻivilaḷ eṉiṉ iṉpa nukarcci emmoṭu koṇmō eṉṟatu, nī varuntuṇiyum ivaḷ āṟṟiyuḷaḷāyiṉ emmoṭu nukarcci koḷvīrāmiṉ eṉa maṟuttavāṟu.
uri :
peruma, eripōlum niṟattiṉik koṇṭa pūviṉi yuṭiya ilavamarattiṉiṉṟum utirnta palavākiya malarkaḷ poripōlum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya puṉkamarattiṉ puḷḷi poruntiya niḻaṟkaṇ kiṭantu aḻakuṟuttum kuḷirnta cevviyiyuṭiya vēṉiṟkālattu viḷiyāṭṭiṉkaṭ peṟum iṉpanukarcci, aḻakum meṉmiyumuṟṟa kūntali yuṭiya ivaḷ niṉ pirivāṟṟi mēṉinalaṉ aḻiviṉṟi yiruppaḷāyiṉ, emmoṭu koḷka eṉṟavāṟu.
ilavamarattiṉ cempū neruppuppōlat tōṉṟuvatupaṟṟi erippū ilavam eṉṟār; piṟarum  “ilavattu, aḻal akin taṉṉa alaṅkuciṉi yoṇpū 1” eṉpar. puṉkamarattiṉ pū veḷḷiya poripōṟaliṉ “porippūm puṉku” eṉpar; “poriyeṉap puṉkaviḻ akaṉṟuṟi 2” “porippūm puṉkiṉ eḻiṟṟaki yoṇmuṟi 3” eṉac cāṉṟōr kuṟippar. ili nerukkam iṉmiyiṉ, pukar niḻal eṉappaṭṭatu. puṉkiṉ  puḷḷi poruntiya nīḻal karumiyum veṇmiyum porunti yiruttalāl iṭiyiṭiyē ilavampū utirntu kiṭappatu paṉṉiṟaṅ kaḷāl nīḻaliṭam oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭatu pōṟa liṉ, varikkum eṉṟār. vēṉiṟkālattu iṉpa nukarccikkut taṇpatam iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉ, taṇpata vēṉil iṉpa nukarcci eṉṟal vēṇṭiṟṟu. vēṉil iṉpam, vēṉiṟ kālattut taṇṇiya poḻiliṭattē cuṟṟam cūḻa iruntuṇṭu makiḻum vēṉilviḻā. āṟāṭṭum vēṉilviḻāvum cēra nāṭṭil mikka ciṟappuṭaṉ nikaḻvaṉa; avaṟṟi nēriṟ kaṇṭa paraṇar, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉip pāṭumiṭattu, “niṉmalip piṟantu niṉkaṭal maṇṭum, malipuṉal nikaḻtarum tīnīr viḻavu” eṉṟu āṟāṭṭiyum, “poḻilvati vēṉiṟ pēreḻil vāḻkki 1” eṉṟu vēṉilviḻāviyum kuṟikkiṉṟār. ivvēṉil iṉ pattip pērūrkku aṇimiyil kāñciyāṟṟiṉ kariyil cuṟṟamoṭu kūṭi nukarnta ciṟappip patiṟṟuppattuc ciṟappik kiṉṟatu. cēranāṭṭiṉa rātaliṉ āntiyār ataṉi īṇṭuk kuṟikkiṉṟār.
piriyak karutum talimakaṉ vēṉiṟparuvattu iṉpa nukarc cikku varuval eṉat tāṉ mīṇṭuvarum paruvattik kuṟittā ṉākaliṉ, ataṉit tōḻi ilavattiṉ paṉmalar puṉkiṉ pukarniḻal varikkum taṇpata vēṉil iṉpa nukarcci eṉṟu ciṟappittāḷ. iṉpakkālattiṟ kātalar piriyā tuṟitali makaḷir peritum virumpupa. kārttiki viḻāviṟ kātalar varutal vēṇṭum eṉat talimakaḷ virumputali, “maṟukuviḷak kuṟuttu māli tūkkip, paḻaviṟal mūtūrp palaruṭaṉ tuvaṉṟiya, viḻa vuṭaṉ ayara varukatil amma 2” eṉa nakkīrar pāṭuvatu kāṇka.
ivvaṇṇam vēṉil iṉpattic ciṟappittavaḷ, ataṉi vaṟpuṟuttip piriviyāyiṉ, talimakaḷ niṉ pirivāṟṟātu mēṉinalam iḻantu aḻivaṉ eṉpāḷ, ammel ōti aḻi vilaḷ eṉiṉ eṉṟum, eṉavē, aḻital orutali yeṉṟum, piṉpu nī mīṇṭu pōntu peṟakkarutum iṉpa nukarccikku iṭamilli yām eṉṟum kūṟuvāḷ, vēṟuvāyp paṭṭāl aḻivilaḷ eṉiṉ emmoṭu koṇmō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
eṉavē, vēṉiṟkālattu iṉpanukarcciyiṉip peṟutaṟku nī mīṇṭu varuntuṇiyum ivaḷ uyirvāḻāḷākaliṉ, nī atu peṟutal ākātu eṉac celavu maṟuttavāṟām.
	369.	vaḷamalar tatinta vaṇṭupaṭu naṟumpoḻil
muḷiniri muṟuva loruttiyoṭu nerunal
kuṟinī ceytaṉi yeṉpa valarē
kurava nīḷciṉi yuṟiyum
paruva mākkuyiṟ kavviyiṟ peritē.
itu, paratti yoruttiyuṭaṉ poḻilakattut taṅki vanta talimakaṉ, talimakaḷ viṉāviyavaḻi, “yāriyum aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟāṉāka, avaḷ kūṟiyatu.
paḻiya uri :
itu pālikku urittākiya vēṉiṟkaṇ nikaḻum kuravum kuyilum kūṟutalāṟ pāliyāyiṟṟu.
uri :
vaḷaviya malarkaḷ niṟintiruttaliṉ vaṇṭukaḷ paṭi yum naṟiya poḻiliṉkaṇ, muḷipōlum niripaṭa niṉṟa paṟkaḷiyuṭiya oruttiyōṭu nī kuṟicey toḻukiṉi yeṉṟu kūṟupa. ataṉvaḻi eḻunta alar, kurava marattiṉ nīṇṭa kiḷiyiṉkaṇ vatiyum vēṉiṟparuvattuk kūvum iyalpiṟṟāya kuyil ceyyum ōciyiṉum peritāyirā niṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.
vaḷamalar tatital kūṟavē vaṇṭu moyttal peṟap paṭutaliṉ, vaṇṭupaṭu poḻil eṉṟār. tatital, neruṅ kutal. paṭutal, īṇṭu moyttal. muḷiniri muṟuval eṉṟatu, muḷipōlum muṟuval, nirimuṟuval eṉa iyiyum. “muḷiniri muṟuvalār 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa. muṟuval, pal. kuṟi, īṇṭup paratti mārpil talivaṉuṭiya cāntum pūṇum ceyyum kuṟi. “niṉ cāntiṉāṟ kuṟikoṇṭāḷ, niṉ mārpiṉ pūṇiṉāṟ kuṟikoṇṭāḷ 1” eṉpatu kāṇka. vēṉiṟ paruvat til kuravamarattiṉ pūñciṉiyi liruntu kuyil kūvuvatu iyalpu. “koḻuntu muntuṟīik kuravarum piṉavē, puṇarntīr puṇar miṉōveṉa iṇarmicic, ceṅkaṇ iruṅkuyil etirkural payiṟṟum 2” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr urippatu kāṇka.
“yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉum kāvu māṭip, patiyikantu  nukartalum uriya eṉpa 3” eṉpavākaliṉ, vaṇṭupaṭu naṟumpoḻil kūṟiṉāḷ.
talimakaṉ aṟiyēṉ eṉpuḻi, kuṟiceyta iṭam muta lāyiṉa yāvum viḷaṅka aṟintu alar kūṟuvōr palar eṉpāḷ, kuṟinī ceytaṉi yeṉpa eṉṟum, nī kūṟā toḻiyiṉum yāṉ avaḷi aṟivēṉ eṉpāḷ, muḷiniri muṟuval orutti eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭāṅkum aṟṟē 4” eṉpataṉāl, talimakaṉ poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu alarkku ētu vātaliṉ, alar..... mākkuyiṟ kavviyiṟ peritē eṉṟāḷ.
iḵtu ūṭaṟ poruṇmittēṉum: vēṉiṟkālattu nikaḻum kuyiṟ kurali uvamittaliṉ pālit tiṇiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum. itu pālikkaṇ marutam nikaḻntatu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum 5 kūṟuvar.
	370.	vaṇciṉik kōṅkiṉ ṟaṇkamaḻ paṭali
iruñciṟi vaṇṭiṉ peruṅkiḷi moyppa
nīnayan tuṟiyap paṭṭōḷ
yāva ḷōvem maṟiyā tīmē.
itu, paratti yoruttikkup pūvaṇintāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭa tali
makaḷ, aḵtu illi eṉṟu maṟikkum6 talimakaṟkuk kūṟiyatu.
paḻiya uri :
itu pālikkuriya karupporuḷākiya kōṅku kūṟiṉamiyāl pāliyāyiṟṟu.
uri :
vaḷaviya kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya kōṅkiṉ taṇṇitāy maṇaṅkamaḻum māliyiṉik kariya ciṟakukaḷi yuṭiya vaṇṭukaḷiṉ periya tokutikaḷ moyttuniṟpa, niṉṉāl aṇintu virumpappaṭṭavaḷ yāvaḷ eṉpataṉi maṟiyātu eṉakku urippāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
paṭali, māli. putuppūkkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa māli eṉṟaṟkut taṇkamaḻ māli eṉṟār. peruntiraḷāṉa vaṇṭu kaḷik kuṟittaṟkup peruṅkiḷi eṉpa. “tēṉcey peruṅ kiḷi 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka. uṟiyap paṭṭōḷ, uṟitaṟku ētuvāyk kātalikkappaṭṭavaḷ. maṟiyātīmē, muṉṉili muṟṟu viṉittiricol.
palar kāṇap parattiyoruttikku māli aṇintu makiḻvittaṉi yākaliṉ, nī eṉṉi maṟitta lākātu eṉpati uḷḷuṟuttu, iruñciṟi vaṇṭiṉ peruṅkiḷi moyppa eṉṟum, avaḷ perunala muṭiya ḷātaliṉ, avaḷi yāṉ kāṇṭaṟku virumpukiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ, yāvaḷō em maṟi yātīmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taṉvayiṉ urimi tōṉṟak kūṟutaliṉ taṉittaṉmip paṉmiyāl yām eṉṟāḷ.

38. makaṭpōkkiyavaḻit tāy iraṅku pattu
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talimakaḷ talimakaṉōṭu puṇarntu uṭaṉēkiyavaḻit tāy, makaḷ pirivāṟṟātu iraṅkik kūṟum pakutiyākaliṉ, iḵtu ippeyarttāyiṟṟu. ippattiṉkaṇ mutal iraṇṭu pāṭṭukkaḷ cila ēṭukaḷil illi eṉpar.
akaṉintiṇik kātaṟkāma vāḻkkiyil talimip paṇpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum ciṟantu niṟparāyiṉum, talimakaṭkut tōḻi pōlac ceviliyum ōraḷavu ciṟappuṭiya ḷāvaḷ. tāy eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ cevili naṟṟāy eṉṟa iruvarum uriyarē; āyiṉum, kaḷavukkuc ceviliyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ taliviyi īṉṟa naṟṟāykkut tōḻiyāyt taliviyip piṟanta nāḷmutal vaḷartta perumi yuṭiyavaḷ. talimakaḷpāl kāṇappaṭum kaṟpum kāmamum naṟpāloḻukkamum piṟavumākiya māṇpukaṭkuc ceviliyum uriyaḷāvaḷ. ataṉāl avaṭkut talivipālum talivikku avaḷpālum neruṅkiya aṉpuṇṭu. kāmam ciṟattalāl talivi talimakaṉoṭu uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi avaḷatu pirivu cevilikku mikka varuttatti viḷittuviṭukiṟatu. talimakaḷit tēṭi ūreṅkum nāṭeṅkum tiriyum ceviliyarum uḷar.
iṉi, talimakaḷatu kātalvāḻvik kuṟitturikkum akapporuṇūl avaḷatu kaṟpumāṇpi eṭutturikkiṟatu; avaḷuṭiya aṟivu ceṟivu arumi mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟi vikkiṟatu. avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattukku aṭikōliya iḷamic cevviyiyum eṭuttōtum kaṭami āciriyarkaṭku varukiṟatu. avarkaḷ ataṉik kāṭṭaṟkuc ceviliyiyum tāyiyum vāyilākak koḷvar. ātalāl akapporuṇūlil tāyarkkuc ciṟanta iṭam uṇmi peṟappaṭum.
uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻit taliviyiṉ cevilikkup perun tuyaram uṇṭākiṟatu. avaḷatu uḷḷattil talimakaḷuṭiya kuṇañceyalkaḷ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa; neñcak kiḻiyil taliviyatu kuḻavipparuvam mutal tavaḻntu moḻipayiṉṟu taḷarnaṭi koṇṭu vaḷarntatum piṟavum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa; taliviyiṉ uṇavum uṭiyum uriyum viḷiyāṭṭum urukkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. maṉakkaṇṇiṟ kāṇum ceviliyiṉ ciṟanta aṟivu avaṟṟic cevviyāka uruppaṭutti veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
talimakaṭkuc ciṟuparuvattē pāluṇavu tarappaṭukiṟatu. pālil tēṉ kalantu poṉvaḷḷattiṟ peytu tāyar uṇpik kiṉṟaṉar. ciṟitu uṇṭatum aḵtu avaṭkut teviṭṭavum, avaḷ uṇṇa maṟuttu iṅkumaṅkum ōṭukiṉṟāḷ; avaḷ piṉṉē ceṉṟu, “kūntal uḷari moḻimi 1” kūṟit tāyar ūṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. talivi ciṟitu vaḷarntapiṉ pālil poriyi ūṟavittut tēṉkalantu koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. 2 pāluṇavi, oṉṟu tāykkuriyatu, oṉṟu tantik kuriyatu, oṉṟu eṉakkuriyatu eṉak kūṟupāṭu ceytu, “eṉpāṭu uṇṭaṉi; tantipāṭu uṇka eṉac colli uṇpittal 1 oruvaki. uṇṭali maṟuttu ōṭuṅkāl accuṟuttalum orōvaḻi uṇṭu.
iḷamakaḷirkkup potuvāka uṭukkum uṭiyiṉmēl ciṟappāka vayalippiṇiyalum alkulum (mēkali) puṉi var. muṉkiyil vaḷiyum kālil aripeyta cilampum aṇiyap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. talimayiri neypeytu vārip pūvittup piṉṉiviṭuvar.
maṉimuṉṟilil iṭṭa maṇaṟpantarilum, paṭappiyi luḷḷa noccivēliyiṉ nīḻalilum viḷiyāṭum talimakaṭku maṉippuṟattiluḷḷa teṟṟiyum maṉinaṭuvaṇ paṭarntuḷḷa vayalip pantarum viḷiyāṭṭiṭamām. pantu kaḻaṅku pāvi mutaliyaṉa taliviyiṉ viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ. taṉṉotta āyamakaḷir puṭicūḻa iruntu viḷiyāṭuvatu taliviyatu pēriyalpu. 2
āṇṭu cila kaḻintum ivviḷiyāṭṭukkaḷōṭu tāḻiyil vayali kuvaḷi mutaliyaṉa vittu vaḷarppatum, kiḷḷi vaḷart talum, pāvikkup paliyūṭṭalum viḷiyāṭṭākiṉṟaṉa.3 poḻilkaḷil pūkkoytalum nīr nilikaḷiṟ paṭintāṭalum kaṭaṟkariyil puṇariyāṭal alavaṉāṭṭal mutaliyaṉavum piṉṉar uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.
talivi taṉ kātalaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi iviyāvum ceviliyiṉ uḷḷattil eḻukiṉṟaṉa. avaḷ ceyta tavaṟukaḷ maṟintu keṭa, avaḷatu meṉmippaṇpu mēlōṅki niṟkiṟatu. avaḷ ceṉṟa vevviya curam niṉivil tōṉṟukiṟatu. veñcurattu veyil vemmiyāl nīrnilikaḷ vaṟṟiviṭuva tāl, āṇṭuk kiṭakkum kalaṅkalnīr cuviyil uvarppā yiruppatum, ataṉi vaḻiccelvōr vēṭki mikutiyāl uṇpatum niṉikkiṉṟāḷ. talimakaḷ ataṉi uṇṇaṟku ettuṇi varuttam koṇṭāḷō eṉa eṇṇi ēṅkukiṟāḷ; appōtu avaḷ taṉ iḷamiyil pāluṇṇātu maṟutta collum veṟutta uḷḷamum koṇṭu oḻukiyatu 1 neñcil tōṉṟit tuyar ceykiṟatu 2. avaḷ ceṉṟavaḻi māmali vilaṅkiya curam eṉpatu aṟintu, “pantuvaḻi yōṭiṉum naṉi nontu vempupavaḷ, vevviya curaneṟiyi evvāṟu naṭantu ceṉṟāḷō?” eṉa eṇṇik kaṇṇīr corivaḷ. “muṉ pellām pāluṇṇāmalum pantāṭāmalum viḷiyāṭāya mōṭu mēvit tirintavaḷ, ippōtu naṭattaṟkariya veṅkāṉam avaṉōṭu celvatu eḷitu eṉa uṇarntāḷ pōlum 3” eṉa varuntukiṉṟāḷ.
talimakaḷatu kaḷavoḻukkam paṟṟi ūril eḻunta alari niṉikkiṉṟāḷ. “ivaḷ kaḷavi, avaḷtāy uvappatō veṟuttut takuvaṉa ceyvatō ceyka eṉa viṭāmal, ampaṟ peṇṭir eṉakku vantu urittaṉar, ataṉik kēṭkiṉ ivaḷ nāṇuvaḷ eṉa yāṉum oṉṟum uriyē ṉāyiṉēṉ. avaḷum ataṉik karantē uṟintā ḷāyiṉum, aṉṉi aṟiyiṉ ivaṇuṟi vāḻkki eḷitākal illi eṉak karuti iḷiyōṉākiya avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟoḻittāḷ4 eṉa ala reḻunta kāli avaḷ oḻukiyatiṟam niṉintu varuntukiṉṟāḷ. piṉṉar oruvāṟu tēṟi, “maṭantikkum kāḷikkum uṇṭākiya naṭpu ivvuṭaṉpōkkāl uṇmiyāyiṟṟu; 5 itu pōtum,” eṉa eṇṇi amipavaḷ, avaṉum avaḷum cellum neṟiyil uḷḷa cīṟūrkkaṇ avarkaḷi varavēṟṟuc celviruntāṟṟit tuccil iruttum maṉikeḻu peṇṭākuka yāṉ 6 eṉa viḻi vatu avaḷatu maṉamāṇpi veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
atuvē cārpāka, avaḷatu tirumaṇam ceviliyiṉ niṉi vukku varukiṟatu. maṉikkup pūvalūṭṭip putumaṇal parappi eṅkaṇum māli cūṭṭit 7 tamar tirumaṇam ceyya muṟ paṭuvatum, āyattārum tāyarum orupāl niṉṟu kāṇac cāṉṟōr muṉṉē niṟpa 1, avaḷuṭiya kiyit talimakaṉ paṟṟum maṇaviṉiyi niṉikkiṉṟāḷ; neñcu kalaṅkukiṟatu; kaṇṇīr āṟāyp perukukiṟatu.
iṉi, taliviyi īṉṟa naṟṟāyum ivvāṟu varuntu kiṉṟāḷ. talimakaḷuṭiya āyamakaḷiriyum ēṉi viḷi yāṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum kāṇuntōṟum, avaṭku avi āṟāt tuyarattic ceyyum. 2 avaḷ perumpālum maṉiyiṉ nīṅkāta māṇpuṭic celviyātaliṉ, maṉiyiṉ akattum puṟattum makaḷ viḷiyāṭiya niṉivu tōṉṟi varutta varuntip piṟarkkum kāṭṭi, “orumakaḷuṭiyēṉ; avaḷum kāḷi yoruvaṉuṭaṉ aruñcuram nerunal ceṉṟāḷ; avaḷ āṭiya teṟṟiyum nocciyum kaṇṭu uḷḷiṉum uḷḷam vēkum 3 ”eṉṟu varuntukiṟāḷ. talimakaḷ ceṉṟa curattiṉ koṭumiyip piṟar kūṟak kēṭpiṉ, “eṉmakaḷ eṉatu aṉpu iyivatāyiṉ, avaḷ cellum vaḻi vampalarit tāṅkum naṉmakkaḷ vāḻum ūrkaḷi uṭiyatākuka 4” eṉṟum, “ñāyiṟu kāyātu maraniḻal porunti maṇal parantu maḻi peytu kuḷirntiruppatāka 5” eṉṟum viḻikiṉṟāḷ.
taṉmaṉikkaṇ uḷḷa celvatti nōkkum naṟṟāy, ataṉip poruḷeṉa eṇṇātu talimakaḷ kaḻintatu avaṭku mikka varuttattic ceykiṟatu. cīrum ciṟappum iṉṟi vaṟumiyil avaḷ kiṭantu varuntuvaḷō eṉṟa vāṭṭam orupāl avaḷ uḷḷatti urukkukiṟatu. 6 avaḷ vaḷartta vayalikkoṭiyik kaṇṭu, “āranī rūṭṭip purappavar, yārmaṟṟup peṟukuvi, aḷiyi nīyē 7” eṉa orupāl avalamuṟuvāḷ. orukāl avaḷ tavaṟu ceytāḷāka, ataṟkāka avaḷi oṟutta taṉ kiyi nōkki, “avaḷi alitta eṉki kaṭunavip paṭuka 8” eṉa nontukoḷvāḷ. makaḷ nīṅkiyatum taṉ uyir nīṅkavilli yeṉa varunti, uyir koḷḷātoḻinta kūṟṟi vekuṇṭu, “māyirun, tāḻi kavippat  tāviṉṟu kaḻika eṟ koḷḷāk  kūṟṟē 1” eṉṟum kūṟuvaḷ.
avaḷpirivi niṉikkumpōtu talimakaṉ niṉivum uṭaṉtōṉṟukiṟatu. makaḷpāṟ cellum aṉpu avaṉpāl ciṉamākiṟatu. taṇinta paruvattiṟ cellātu vetirnel viḷi yum vēṉil veṅkāṉattil koṇṭu talik kaḻin tāṉē 2 eṉak kotikkiṉṟāḷ. makaṭkut tāyākiya eṉṉip pōlavē eṉmakaḷik “koṇṭuṭaṉ pōka valitta, vaṉkaṭ kāḷiyi īṉṟa tāy peruvituppuṟuka 3” eṉa vetumpukiṉṟāḷ.
uṭaṉpōkiya talimakaṭkut talivaṉmaṉiyil tiru maṇam naṭakkum ceyti varak kēṭṭatum avaḷvaṭivu muṟṟum ārvamāy viṭukiṟatu. ammaṭanallāḷi īṉṟamipaṟṟi eṉpāl aruḷāṉāyiṉum, avaḷip paṉṉāḷ kūntal vāri nucuppiṟ cumantu ōmpiya utaviyuṭiyēṉ eṉac colli, vēlaṉi varuvittu, “vēlaṉē, uṉ kaḻaṅku nōkkic col: eṉakku makaḷik kāṇāmiyāl kaṇṇuṟakkam illi; yāṉ iṉitu uṟaṅka avaṉ avaḷi iṅkē koṇarvāṉāyiṉ, avaḷi eṉmaṉi muntuṟak koṇarvāṉō? taṉmaṉikku uyppāṉā, avaṉ kuṟippu yātu? 4” eṉṟu kēṭkiṉṟāḷ.
tirumaṇam niṉivileḻutalum, tirumaṇattil tāyākiya tāṉ taliviyiṉ maṭami pārāṭṭa, avaṉ tāy avaṉatu cemmal kaṇṭu ataṟkuriya ciṟappuc ceyya nikaḻum maṉiviṉi tōṉṟi varuttukiṟatu5. tirumaṇattukku muṉṉaṇiyāka naṭi peṟum cilampukaḻi naṉmaṇam. taṉ maṉikkaṇ nikaḻāmiyip paṉmuṟiyum eṇṇic 6 “cilampu kaḻīiya celvam, piṟaruṇak kaḻintatu 7” eṉak kiyaṟṟu varuntuvāḷ. iṉi varum pāṭṭuk kaḷilum iṉṉōraṉṉa karuttukkaḷik kāṇalām.
	371.	maḷḷar koṭṭiṉ maññi yālum
uyarneṭuṅ kuṉṟam paṭumaḻi taliic
curanaṉi yiṉiya vākuka tilla
aṟaneṟi yituveṉat teḷintaveṉ
piṟinutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pōkiya kāṉē.
itu, talimakaḷ puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiyavaḻi, naṟṟāy “uṭaṉ
pōkku aṟaneṟi” eṉṟu makiḻntu kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam kūṭṭiya nalviṉiyit taṉṉeñciṟku viḷakkip pulampiyatu.
uri :
maḷḷarkaḷ ceyyum muḻakkattukkut taka mayil ālum neṭitā yuyarnta kuṉṟattil olikkiṉṟa mukil maḻi peyta lāl, ataṉic cārnta curam vemmiyē yiṉṟi iṉimi yuṭittākuka, talimakaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkutalākiya iḵtu aṟaneṟiyē eṉat teḷintu ceṉṟa eṉṉuṭiya piṟi pōlum neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya makaḷ pōkiya kāṉa mātalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
maḷḷar, curattiṉkaṇ vāḻum maṟavar. avaratu paṟi muḻakkiṟ kēṟpa mayil tōkiyi virittāṭum eṉṟārāyiṉum, mayil āṭutaṟ kēṟpa maḷḷar paṟikoṭṭuvareṉpatu karuttākak koḷka. ālutal, āṭutal. ālutal akavuta lāyiṉ, maḷḷar koṭṭil eṉṟataṟku maḷḷaratu paṭikkoṭṭiliṉ mēliruntu mayil akavum eṉṟu urikka. peyal karuti muḻaṅkum karu mukilip paṭumaḻi eṉpa; “paṭumaḻi urumiṉ muḻaṅkum 1” eṉpatu kāṇka. curam maḻitalii iṉiya vākuka eṉṟu iyiyum. til, viḻivupporuṭṭu. itu, talimakaṉuṭaṉ koṇṭutalikkaḻiyum ivvoḻukkam. kāṉ, kāṉam. “curaṉ aṇi koṇṭa kāṉam 2” eṉṟatu kāṇka. tēṟṟēkāram vikārattāl tokkatu.
kātalaṉ karuttiṉvaḻi yoḻukum kaṟpu mēmpaṭṭāḷ eṉa makaḷatu ceyalmāṇpiṉi uḷḷuṟuttu urittaliṉ, kaṟ puṭiya peṇṭir irunta nāṭṭil maḻi vaṟaṅkūrātu eṉpatu paṟṟic curam naṉi iṉiyavākuka tilla eṉṟāḷ. maḷḷar koṭṭukkut taka mayil ālum eṉṟatu, talimakaṉ vēṭṭa taṉiyē talimakaḷum vēṭṭoḻukukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu; inneṟimi avaḷpāl illiyāyiṉ uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻātu eṉa aṟika.
talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōtaṟkuk kāraṇam pallāṟṟāṉum ārāyntāṭku, muṉṉi nalviṉi kūṭṭat talimakaṉōṭu uṭaṉceṉṟu kaṟpup pūṇṭu avaṉōṭu nikaḻttum oḻukkamē irumiyum payattaṟ ciṟappuṭiya nallaṟam eṉa uṇarntāḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, aṟaneṟi ituveṉat teḷinta eṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam uṇarum mutukkuṟivuṭiya ḷāyiṉum curattiṉ koṭumiyi āṟṟum matukiyilaḷ eṉpāḷ, piṟi nutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟum, aṟaneṟi yuṇarntu ataṉik kaṭip piṭittuc celvārkku nōytaruvaṉa illi yeṉiṉum, tāymiyāl curattiṉ koṭumi nōkki iraṅkukiṉṟāḷā kaliṉ, curam naṉi iṉiya vākuka eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “iṟanta kaṟpiṉāṭku evvam paṭaraṉmiṉ, ciṟantāṉi vaḻipaṭīic ceṉṟaṉaḷ, aṟantali piriyā āṟumaṟ ṟatuvē 1” eṉṟum, “puṇarntuṭaṉ pōtal poruḷeṉa, uṇarntēṉ maṉṟaavar uṇarā vūṅkē 2” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār piṟarum. tēṟṟēkāram vikārattāl tokkatu. vēṉilāl vevvitāya curam iṉitātaṟ poruṭṭuk kuṉṟam paṭumaḻi taliic, curanaṉi iṉiya vākuka eṉṟatu vāḻttumām; “ñāyiṟu kāyātu maraniḻaṟ paṭṭu, malimutaṟ ciṟuneṟi maṇalmikat tāayt, taṇmaḻi taliyiṉ ṟāka nannīttuc, cuṭarvāy neṭuvēṟ kāḷiyoṭu, maṭamā varivi pōkiya curaṉē 3” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
itaṉuḷ “aṟaneṟi ituveṉat teḷinta eṉmakaḷ” eṉṟu tāy kūṟavē, uṭaṉpōkkut tarumameṉṟu makiḻntukūṟi aṅṅaṉam kūṭṭiya nalviṉiyit taṉ neñciṟku viḷakkip pulampiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 4
	372.	eṉṉu muḷḷiṉaḷ kollō taṉṉi
neñcuṇat tēṟṟiya vañciṉak kāḷiyo
ṭaḻuṅkaṉ mūtū ralareḻac
ceḻumpala kuṉṟa miṟantaveṉ makaḷē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi, naṟṟāy taṉ oḻukkat tāl ūr alareṭuttu urittali niṉiyātu, ceṉṟavaḷ eṉṉiyum uḷḷiṉaḷkollō veṉak kavaṉṟurittatu.
uri :
taṉṉi neñcu koḷḷumāṟu takuvaṉa kūṟit teḷi vittuk koṇṭutalikkaḻinta vañciṉatti yuṭiya kāḷi pōlvāṉuṭaṉ, yāṉ aḻuṅkutaṟ kiṭaṉākiya ippaḻiya vūrār alar kūṟa, ataṉi niṉittalum ceyyātu, ceḻitta palavākiya kuṉṟaṅkaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟa eṉmakaḷ tāṉ pōkiyavaḻiyil eṉṉiyum niṉintāḷ kollō? aṟiyēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
neñcuṇṭal, maṉattuṭ kōṭal. vañciṉam, kaṭuñcūḷ. “eñcā vañciṉam neñcuṇak kūṟi 1” eṉṟār piṟarum. kāḷi pōlvāṉik kāḷi yeṉṟār; itu tiṇinilippeyar. uḷḷāmi yuṇaraniṉṟamiyiṉ, ummi, etirmaṟi. aḻuṅkaṉ mūtūr pēroliyiṉiyuṭiya mūtūr eṉiṉumām; “uṟiviṉi tammaviv vaḻuṅka lūrē 2” eṉṟār piṟarum.
mikka iḷamiyum maṭappamu muṭiyavaḷāyt tōḻiyar tāyar ākiya ivarip piriyātiruntāḷ, pirivacca miṉṟip pōkkuṭaṉpaṭa vēṇṭutaliṉ, taṉṉi neñcuṇat tēṟṟiya eṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam tēṟṟat tēṟiyavaḷ ūrār kūṟum alarkkuc ciṟitum uṭkātu ceṉṟataṉi niṉintu viyappāḷāy, aḻuṅkal mūtūr alareḻa eṉṟum, avvalarurikku nāṇi yām īṇṭu iruntu varuntāniṟpa, aḻuṅkal iyalpāka vuṭiya mūtūr eṭutta alarip poruḷākak koḷḷāmiyiṉ, avvūroṭu ataṉkaṇ uṟiyum emmiyum tuṟantoḻintāḷ pōlum eṉpāḷ, ceḻumpal kuṉṟam iṟanta eṉmakaḷ eṉṟum, etaṉi niṉiyāḷāyiṉum īṉṟu puṟantanta taṉṉi maṟattaṟku ētu oṉṟum kāṇāḷāy, eṉṉi niṉintāḷ kollō veṉṟu taṉ ārāk kātalāl alamarukiṉṟāḷā kaliṉ, eṉṉum uḷḷiṉaḷ kollō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
aṉṉi aṟiyiṉ taṉakku immaṉiyil uṟital tuṉpa mām eṉak karuti nīṅkiṉā ḷākaliṉ, īṇṭu uṟiyum aṉṉi yākiya taṉṉi avaḷ tuṟantālaṉṟi, uṭaṉpōkkukku uḷḷam cellā teṉpatu paṟṟi ivvāṟu niṉintāḷ eṉṟumām. “aṉṉi yaṟiyiṉ ivaṇuṟi vāḻkki, eṉakkeḷi tākal illeṉak kaḻaṟkāl,  miṉṉoḷir neṭuvēl iḷiyōṉ muṉṉuṟap, paṉmali yaruñcuram pōkiya taṉakku 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
ummi kātaṟciṟappuṇara niṉṟamiyiṉ ciṟappumām. ākavē, naṟṟāymāṭṭu niṉital eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. “iḵtu eṉṉi niṉippāḷō eṉṟatu” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 2 itu talimakaḷ koṭumi niṉintu kūṟiya teṉpar iḷampūraṇar 3.
	373.	niṉittoṟuṅ kaliḻu miṭumpi yeytuka
pulikkōṭ piḻitta kavikkōṭṭu mutukali
māṉpiṇi yaṇitara vāṇkural viḷikkum
veñcura meṉmaka ḷuytta
vampami valvil viṭali tāyē.
itu, talimakaḷit talimakaṉ koṇṭukaḻinta koṭumi niṉintu naṟṟāy colliyatu.
paḻiya uri :
ivaṭkāka yāṉ paṭṭa tuyaram avaṉ tāyum paṭavēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
uri :
puliyāṟ koḷḷappaṭutaliṟ piḻitta kavitta kompu kaḷiyuṭiya mutiya kalimāṉ taṉ piṇimāṉ taṉṉi yaṭiyumāṟu āṇkurali yeṭuttaḻikkum vevviya curattiṉkaṇ, eṉmakaḷ cellumāṟu ceyta putiya mūṅki lāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa valiya villiṉiyuṭiya viṭalikkut  tāyākiyavaḷ niṉikkuntōṟum eṉpōl kaṇṇīr vārum tuṉpattiṉi eytuvāḷāka eṉṟavāṟu.
niṉikkuntōṟum neñcu kalaṅkik kaṇṇīr coriyap perukum tuṉpam niṉittoṟum kaliḻum iṭumpi eṉap paṭṭatu. kavikkōṭu, kiḷitta kompukaḷ. puli muḻakkam kēṭṭu añcic citaṟi yōṭiya piṇikkut tāṉ uyntami terivittu aḻikkumāṟu tōṉṟa, māṉpiṇi aṇitara āṇkural viḷikkum eṉṟār. āṇmi tōṟṟuvikkum kural āṇkural eṉappaṭṭatu. atu kēṭṭu māṉiṉam oruṅku tiraḷum eṉa aṟika. “keṭumāṉ iṉaniri tarīiya kaliyē, katirmāy māli āṇkural viḷikkum 1” eṉap piṟarum kūṟupa. ampukkuk kaṭumiyum ciṟappum ataṉic celuttum villiṉ vaṉmimēl niṟṟaliṉ, vampami valvil eṉṟār. vampu, putumi.
taṉ makaḷatu pōkkiṉi niṉiyuntōṟum, curattiṭi vilaṅku puḷ veyil vemmi mutaliyavaṟṟālum, tēṭip piṉceṉṟa tanti taṉṉiyar eṉṟu ivarālum avaḷ tīṅ keytukiṉṟaṉaḷō eṉṉum aiyamum kalakkamum tōṉṟi maṉatti alittaliṉ, niṉittoṟum kaliḻum iṭumpi yeṉṟāḷ.
talimakaḷiṉ tanti taṉṉiyar peruñciṉaṅ koṇṭu cēṟaliṉ avarāl tīṅku viḷiyu meṉa añciṉāḷ; “avarum terikaṇi nōkkic cilinōkkik kaṇcēntu, orupaka lellām urutteḻuntu āṟi, iruvarkaṭ kuṟṟamum illi yāl eṉṟu, terumantu cāyttār tali 2” eṉpatu kāṇka.
itu “naṉmi tīmi accam cārtaleṉ  ṟaṉṉavi piṟavum” eṉpuḻik kūṟiya accam. itaṉkaṇ naṟṟāymāṭṭup poṟāmi yeṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. “viḻavut talik koṇṭa paḻaviṟaṉ mūtūrp, pūṅka ṇāyam kāṇṭoṟum empōṟ, peruvitup puṟuka mātō emmiṟ, pomma lōtiyit taṉ moḻik koḷīik, koṇṭuṭaṉ pōka valitta, vaṉkaṭ kāḷiyi yīṉṟa tāyē1” eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
	374.	pallūḻ niṉippiṉu nalleṉ ṉūḻa
mīḷi muṉpiṟ kāḷi kāppa
muṭiyakam pukāak kūntalaḷ
kaṭuvaṉu maṟiyāk kāṭiṟan tōḷē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻi avaḷiḷami niṉintu iraṅkit tāy kūṟiyatu.
uri :
palamuṟi ārāyiṉum ivvuṭaṉpōkku naṉmuṟiyē yeṉṟu karuti, mīḷipōlum valiyiṉiyuṭiya kāḷipōl vāṉ kāval ceyya, muṭiyiṭutaṟkuriya nīṭci peṟāta kūntali yuṭiyavaḷākiya eṉmakaḷ, kuraṅkukaḷālum pukuntaṟiyap paṭāta kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉaḷ; avaḷatu aṟivirunta vāṟu eṉ? eṉṟavāṟu.
pallūḻ, paṉmuṟi; “pallūḻ noṭitteṉac civanta melviral. 2” nalleṉ ūḻa, nalla eṉappaṭum ūḻ; nallūḻ eṉṟavāṟu; eṉṟeṉpatu eñci niṉṟatu. ivvūḻ iruvarukkum ottaliṉ potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār. mīḷi muṉpiṉ kāḷi, kūṟṟi yotta valiyiṉiyuṭiya kāḷi. mīḷi, kūṟṟu. peṇmic cevvi eytuṅ kālattu makaḷirkkuk kūntal naṉku muṭittaṟkuriya vaḷarcci peṟum eṉpatu paṟṟi muṭiyakam pukāak kūntal eṉṟār; “talimuṭi cāṉṟa taṇṭaḻi yuṭiyi. alamaral āyamoṭu yāṅkaṇum paṭāal 3” eṉa iṟceṟikku māṟṟālum iḵtu aṟiyappaṭum. kaṭuvaṉum aṟiyāk kāṭu eṉṟatu, “mantiyu maṟiyā marampayi laṭukkam 4” eṉṟāṟ pōla. ummi, ciṟappu.
emmuṟiyāṉ ārāyiṉum, makaḷir uṟṟārkkē uriya reṉṉum naṉmuṟiyē peṟappaṭutaliṉ, pallūḻ niṉip piṉum nalleṉ ṉūḻa eṉṟum, kūntalum naṉku vaḷarāta iḷami yuṭiyava ḷeṉṟaṟku, muṭiyakam pukāak kūntalaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itu taṉmi yeṉṉum meyppāṭām. iṉi, kāṭiṟantōḷ ceyal eṉa oru col varuvittu nalla veṉṟa muṟiyiṉavām eṉṟu muṭittalumām. āyiṉum, avaḷ muṭiyakam pukāk kūntalaḷeṉa ākkam varuvittu urippiṉum amiyum. paṉmi vāypāṭṭāṟ kūṟiyatu, aṟivaṟitalum añcātu cēṟalum nōkki eṉka. taṉ makaḷ kāṭu iṟantamik kuriya kāraṇattip paṉmuṟi nōkki avaḷatu aṟivu viḷaṅkak kaṇṭu amintā ḷāyiṉum, muṭiyakam pukāk kūntalaḷ eṉa avaḷatu iḷamic cevvi niṉiviṉkaṇ nilavutal paṟṟi, “iḷami niṉintu kūṟiyatu” eṉat tuṟi kūṟiṟṟu. piṟāṇṭum, makaḷ kūntal muṭiyakam pukāmi niṉintu, “cantuvaḷar vaṇar kural vāri vakivakuttu, yāṉpōtu tuṇippat takaram maṇṇāḷ, taṉṉō raṉṉa takiveṅ kātalaṉ, veṟikamaḻ paṉmalar puṉiyap piṉṉuviṭac, ciṟupuṟam putiya neṟipu tāḻntaṉa kol 1” eṉat tāy varuntik kūṟumāṟu kāṇka.
	375.	ituveṉ pāvi pāvi yituveṉa
alamaru nōkki ṉalamvaru cuṭarnutaṟ
piṅkiḷi yeṭutta piṅkiḷi yeṉṟivi
kāṇṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟuṅ kalaṅka
nīṅkiṉa ḷōveṉ pūṅka ṇōḷē.
itu, cēriyum ayalum tēṭik kāṇātu vantārik kaṇṭu talimakaḷtāy colliyatu.
uri :
ippāvi eṉ pāvipōlvāḷ vittāṭum pāvi yākum eṉṟum, ikkiḷi cuḻaṉṟa nōkkattiyuṭiya aḻaku mikka oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya paciya kiḷḷi pōlvāḷ vaḷartta paciya kiḷi eṉṟum kūṟi, yāṉ ivaṟṟik kāṇuntōṟum kalaṅki varunta, eṉ aḻakiya kaṇkaḷi yuṭiya makaḷ pirintu ceṉṟaṉaḷ pōlum. eṉṟavāṟu.
mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa pāviyum piṅkiḷiyum talimakaḷik kuṟippaṉa. alamaral, cuḻalutal. nalam, aḻaku. eṉṟivi, eṉṟa ivi; akaram vikārattāl tokkatu. kāṇum kaṇ kalaṅki nīrcorivatu paṟṟi, kalaṅka eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār. pūṅkaṇ, pūppōlum kaṇ; “pū veṉ orupeyar āyiyal piṉṟē, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē 1” eṉṟami yiṉ, melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭintatu; “pūṅkaṇ āyam 2” eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
talimakaṉōṭu kaḷavuneṟikkaṇ oḻukuṅkāl taṉ ceyal maṉiyavarkkup pulaṉākumō eṉak kaṇantōṟum añci alamantu oḻukiṉāḷākaliṉ, avaḷatu pārviyi, alamaru nōkku eṉṟum, talimakaṉ kūṭṭattāl taliviyiṉ cuṭar nutal aḻakumikkut tikaḻntami tōṉṟa, nalam varu cuṭar nutal eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.
taṉ aṉpuṭiya makaḷip pirintavaḷ, avaḷ virumpip pōṟṟiya pāviyum kiḷḷiyum kāṇuntōṟum avaḷ niṉiveḻuntu varuttutaliṉ, kāṇṭoṟum kāṇṭoṟum kalaṅka eṉṟum, tēṭic ceṉṟār avaḷniliyaṟintu varuva reṉṟu eṇṇiyiruntavaḷ, aṅṅaṉam vārāmiyāl nīṅkiṉaḷō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. payiliṭamum, payil poruḷum taṉittaṉi nōkkuntōṟum varuttutaliṉ, kāṇṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟum eṉa aṭukkik kūṟiṉāḷ. itaṉāl tāypāl araṟṟu eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟutal kāṇka.
talivi paliyiṭṭup paraviyatu pāvi; pālūṭṭi vaḷart tatu kiḷḷi. pāvi palipeṟāmiyum kiḷḷi pāluṇṇā miyum kaṇṭu varuntiyavāṟu; “centārk kiḷḷiyum tīmpāl uṇṇā, kēḻkoḷak kāḻpuṇarntiyaṟṟiya vaṉappami nōṉcuvarp, pāviyum paliyeṉap peṟāa nōypora, ivikaṇ ṭiṉivataṉ ṟaliyum niṉivilēṉ 1” eṉat tāṉ kalaṅki moḻivatu kāṇka.
itu tēṭikkāṇātu vantārik kaṇṭu pulampiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 2. itu cēriyōri viṉāyatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar 3.
iṉi, “ituveṉ pāvik kiṉiyanaṉ pāvi, yituveṉ piṅkiḷi eṭutta piṅkiḷi, yituveṉ pūvik kiṉiyacoṟ pūviyeṉ, ṟalamvaru nōkki ṉalamvaruñ cuṭarnutal, kāṇ ṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟuṅ kalaṅki, nīṅkiṉa ḷōveṉ pūṅka ṇōḷē” eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉa, 4 naṉpāvi yeṉṟum, piṅkiḷi yeṉṟum, coṟpūvi yeṉṟum, kāṇṭoṟuṅ kāṇṭoṟuṅ kalaṅka, cuṭar nutaliyuṭiyaḷākiya pūṅka ṇōḷ nīṅkiṉaḷō veṉṟu ceyteṉeccattit tirittu muṭitturikka.
	376.	nāṭoṟuṅ kaliḻu meṉṉiṉu miṭiniṉṟu
kāṭupaṭu tīyiṟ kaṉaliyar mātō
nalviṉi neṭunakar kalleṉak kalaṅkap
pūppuri yuṇkaṇ maṭavaraṟ
pōkkiya puṇartta vaṟaṉil pālē.
itu, talimakaḷ pōyavaḻi naṟṟāy vitiyi vekuṇṭu colliyatu.
uri :
nalla ceyviṉi aminta neṭiya maṉikkaṇ uṟiyum namar kalleṉṟu kalaṅki varuntap pūppōlum miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya maṭappam poruntiya makaḷ pōtaṟku ūkkiya aṟappaṇpillāta tīviṉippāl, nāṭōṟum yāṉ kaluḻntu varuntumāṟu pōla, kāṭṭiṭiyē niṉṟu ataṉ kaṇ tōṉṟiya tīyiṭippaṭṭu erintoḻiyumāka eṉṟavāṟu.
iṭi, curattiṉ iṭinilam. kaṉalutal, īṇṭu ventu keṭutal. nalviṉi, nallavēlippāṭu. iṉi, nalviṉip payaṉāka viḷiyum celvamum iṉpamum poruntiya nukarccik kaminta perumaṉi eṉṟaṟku nalviṉi neṭunakar eṉṟumām. nakar, vīṭu, īṇṭu ākupeyarāl nakarkkaṇ uṟiyum tamarmēl niṉṟatu. puṇarttal, niṉikkappaṇṇic ceyaliṭi oṉṟu vittal. aṟaṉ, īṇṭu aruḷaṟam kuṟittu niṉṟatu.
talimakaḷi viṭātu toṭarntu cēṟaṟkaṇ taṉṉeñcum pālum ottamiyiṉ, eṉṉiṉum eṉṟum, ciṟitum eñcāmal ventu nīṟupaṭṭaḻital kuṟippātaliṉ kāṭu paṭu tī eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
celva neṭumaṉikkaṇ iṉitirunta taṉmakaḷ ataṉiṉ nīṅki veñcurattuc cēṟaṟkuk kāraṇam ūḻ eṉa ōrntu urippāḷ, pōkkiya puṇartta eṉṟum, aṟamallavi oṉṟum ceytaṟiyārkku avaṟṟic ceytārkku eytaṟ pālatāya tuṉpatti eytuvittal, pāl vari teyvattiṟkup pāṅkaṉṟu eṉpāḷ, aṟaṉil pālē eṉṟum, ataṉāl ataṉi āṟṟa oṟuttal āvatē yeṉṉum karuttāl, kāṭupaṭu tīyiṟ kaṉaliyar mātō eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. kūṟavē, tāymāṭṭu uyirp peṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. nalviṉip payaṉākap peṟṟa neṭumaṉi vāḻkkiyil irātu tīviṉip payaṉāka veñcuram naṭantu varuntac ceytal paṟṟi ūḻviṉip pāli, aṟaṉil pāl eṉṉum nayam kāṇka.
“itu tīviṉiyi vekuṇṭu pulampiyavāṟu kāṇka. pāl, paḻaviṉi” eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 1.
	377.	nīrnacik kūkkiya vuyaval yāṉi iyampuṇar tūmpi ṉuyirkku mattam ceṉṟaṉaṇ maṉṟaveṉ makaḷē pantum pāviyuṅ kaḻaṅkumemak koḻittē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōyavaḻi avaḷ pantu mutalākiya kaṇṭa naṟṟāy kalaṅkic colliyatu.
uri :
nīrvēṭki mītūrntu varuntum yāṉi vaṅkiyam eṉṉum icikkaruviyiṉ tūmpupōlat tutikkiyiṉi yuyarttip piḷiṟum vaḻiyiṉkaṇ, eṉmakaḷ, pantum pāviyum kaḻaṅkum mutalākap pala viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum yāṉ kaṇṭu varuntumāṟu viṭṭuvittuc ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
uyaval, varuttam. ūkkiya yāṉi yeṉka. “nīrnacik kūkkiya uyaval yāṉi 1” eṉap piṟarum kūṟupa. nīrnacikku ūkkiya yāṉi eṉpataṟku nīrvēṭkiyāṉ atu peṟutali mēṟkoṇṭa yāṉi eṉiṉumām. “uṭalunar uṭka vīṅkik kaṭaleṉa, vāṉnīrkku ūkkum tāṉi 2” eṉpataṉāl aṟika.
nīrvēṭki mikku ataṉip peṟutalik karuti uḷḷam eḻuntatāyiṉum, uṭal taḷarntu cellamāṭṭāmiyiṉ varuntumāṟu tōṉṟa uyaval yāṉi eṉṟār. palvaki iyaṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyē icikkappaṭuvatu paṟṟi neṭuvaṅkiyam īṇṭu, iyampuṇar tūmpu eṉappaṭṭatu. “pāṭiṉ teṇkiṇi kaṟaṅkak...... kuṟuneṭun  tūmpoṭu muḻavuppuṇarn  ticippat..... tēri yoliyiṉ māṇac cīramittuc, cillari kaṟaṅkum ciṟupalliyam 3” eṉpatu kāṇka. tūmpu yāṉiyiṉ kipōlum eṉpati “ōykaḷi ṟeṭutta nōyuṭi neṭuṅki, tokucoṟ kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum, attam 4” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvar.
viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷi virumpip pōṟṟum iḷami kaḻintu aṟivaṟiyum cevvi yeytit taṉ kaṭaṉ aṟintu uṭaṉ pōkkut tuṇintaṉaḷākaliṉ, ceṉṟaṉaḷ maṉṟa eṉ makaḷē eṉṟum, taṉmakaḷ pēṇiya poruḷkaḷit tāṉ kāṇṭoṟum varuttam mikutaliṉ, pantum pāviyuṅ kaḻaṅkum emakku oḻittē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. piṟarum, “uḷḷiṉ uḷḷam vēmē uṇkaṇ, maṇivāḻ pāvi naṭikaṟ ṟaṉṉa eṉ, aṇi mayiṟ kuṟumakaḷ āṭiya, maṇiyēr nocciyum teṟṟiyum kaṇṭē 5” eṉavum kūṟutal kāṇka.
“pāviyum pantum pavaḷavāyp piṅkiḷiyum, āyamum oṉṟum iviniṉiyāḷ pālpōlum, āynta moḻiyiṉāḷ celluṅkol kātalaṉpiṉ, kāyntu katirteṟūuṅ kāṭu 1” eṉavum kūṟutal kāṇka.
	378.	celliya muyalip pāaya ciṟakar
vāva lukakku māliyām pulampap
pōkiya vavaṭkō nōvēṉ ṟēmoḻit
tuṇiyilaḷ kaliḻu neñciṉ
iṇiyē ruṇka ṇivaṭkunō vatuvē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷtōḻi āṟṟāmi kaṇṭa naṟṟāy colliyatu.
uri :
cellutaṟku muyaṉṟu parappiya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya vāval virumpic cellum mālikkālattu, yām pulampeytumāṟu emmip pirintu ceṉṟa makaḷi niṉintu aḻuvēṉallēṉ; iṉiya moḻiyiṉiyuṭiya tuṇiviyi yiṉṟi varunti iṉiyum neñciṉōṭu iṇiyotta miyuṇṭa kaṇ
kaḷiyuṭiya tōḻiyākiya ivaḷ poruṭṭē pulampuvē
ṉāyiṉēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
pakaṟkālattuc cēṟalākāmiyiṉ, celliya muyalip pāya ciṟakar eṉṟum, mālippoḻutē vāvaṟku iri tērtaṟkuriya kālamākaliṉ, vāval ukakkum māli eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. “piyuḷ mālip paḻumaram paṭariya, novvuppaṟi vāval 2” eṉpa. avaḷ, ivaḷ eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭukkaḷ muṟiyē tali
makaḷiyum tōḻiyiyum kuṟittaṉa; ceyyuḷātaliṉ cuṭṭu muṟpaṭa vantatu. ōkāram  ciṟappu. nōvēṉ: kuṟippāl, nōtaliṉ mikuti yuṇarttiṟṟu, “eṉmakaḷ kaḻintataṟku aḻin
taṉṟō vilaṉ 3” eṉṟāṟ pōla. tēmoḻittuṇi, tīviya moḻi vaḻaṅkum tuṇivi.
pakaṟpōtil paranta ulakiṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum nikaḻccikaḷil virintu niṟkum maṉamum kaṇṇum mālippōtil curuṅki maṉiyakattē oṭuṅki niṟṟalāṉ, akkālattē pirintōratu pirivu peritum varuttumātaliṉ, māli yām pulampap pōkiya avaḷ eṉṟāḷ. “niṉtōḻi, tirunakar varippakam pulampa avaṉoṭu, perumali yiṟantatu nōvēṉ 1” eṉat tāy piṟāṇṭum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
uṭaṉiruntu uṭaṉvaḷarntu uṭaṉviḷiyāṭi iṉmoḻi vaḻaṅki iṉpuṟuttiya tōḻiyākiya taliviyip pirintu yāroṭum collāṭātu taṉittiruntu varuntutaliṉ, tēmoḻit tuṇiyilaḷ kaliḻum eṉṟum, taṉṉiṉum tōḻi eytum varuttam peritātal kāṇṭaliṉ, iṇiyēr uṇkaṇ ivaṭku nōvatuvē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
tāṉuṟu tuyarattiṉum piṟar tuyaram tuṭittal takavuṭit teṉṉum muṟimiyāl ivaṭku nōvatuvē eṉṟāḷ eṉiṉumām. iḵtu aruḷ eṉṉum meyppāṭu. itu tōḻi tēettup pulampal eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 2
	379.	taṉṉama rāyamoṭu naṉmaṇa nukarcciyiṉ
iṉitāṅ kollō taṉakkē paṉivari
iṉakkaḷiṟu vaḻaṅkuñ cōli
vayakkuṟu veḷvē lavaṟpuṇarntu celavē.
itu, puṇarntuṭaṉpōkiyavaḻit tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟpak kēṭṭa naṟṟāy, “ataṉi muṉṉē aṟivittu nām maṇam puṇartta oḻukātu pōyiṉaḷ” eṉa nontu colliyatu.
uri :
kuḷirnta malikkaṇṇē kūṭṭamākiya yāṉikaḷ iyaṅkum cōlikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē, viḷakkam mikka veṉṟi tarum vēliyuṭiyavaṉōṭu puṇarntuṭaṉcēṟal, taṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa āyamakaḷir cūḻa nām puṇarkkum naṉmaṇat tiṭip peṟalākum iṉpanukarcciyiṉum iṉitākuṅ kollō? eṉṟavāṟu.
celavu, nukarcciyiṉ iṉitāṅ kollō eṉak kūṭṭuka. amartal, virumputal. iṉ: oppupporuṭṭu; uṟaḻcciyu
mām. taṉakkeṉṟatu, talimakaṭku. puṇarnteṉṉum cey teṉeccam celaveṉṉum viṉippeyariṭattu viṉi kāṇ
ṭatu. kol: aiyam. ōkāram: acinili. vaṭittuk kūritākki ney taṭavappeṟṟuṇmiyiṉ, veḷvēl eṉappaṭṭatu.
talimakaṭkut talimakaṉōṭu uḷatākiya naṭpiṉi muṉṉē taṉakku aṟivittut tāṉ maṇam puṇartta oḻukātu pōyiṉā ḷeṉṉuṅ karuttāl, peṟṟōrum uṭaṉpayiṉṟa āyattōrum oruṅku kūṭi nukarvikkum iṉpattiṉum, ava
rallāta putuvōr puṇarkkum iṉpam ciṟappuṭittaṉṟu eṉpāḷ, iṉitāṅ kollō eṉṟāḷ.
“taṉṉō raṉṉa āyamum mayiliyal, eṉṉō raṉṉa tāyarum kāṇa....... poṉṉuṭi neṭunakarp puriyōr ayara, naṉmāṇ viḻaviṉ takaram maṇṇi, yām pala puṇarppac collāḷ 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. maṇattiṭip peṟum nukarcciyē iṉiteṉṉum karuttiṉa ḷātaliṉ, naṉ
maṇam eṉṟāḷ. naṉmaṇa nukarcci pōlap puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya celavum iṉitāyiṉ pōtal vēṇṭā; pōtalāl īṇṭiya maṇanukarcciyiṉum āṇṭiya iṉimi peritām pōlum eṉpatu eytutal kaṇṭu, iṉitām kol eṉṟāḷ eṉka.
	380.	atta nīḷiṭi yavaṉoṭu pōkiya
muttēr veṇpaṉ mukiḻnaki maṭavaral
tāya reṉṉum peyarē vallā
ṟeṭuttēṉ maṉṟa yāṉē
koṭuttōr maṉṟavava ḷāyat tōrē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōkiyavaḻit teruṭṭuvārkkuc cevilittāy kūṟiyatu.
uri :
nīṇṭa vaḻiyiṉiyuṭiya curattiṉkaṇ avaṉoṭu puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya muttup pōṉṟa veḷḷiya paṟkaḷi
yum, mukiḻtta nakiyiṉiyumuṭiya maṭavaralākiya talimakaṭkut tāyar eṉṉum peyari yāṉ eṉṉāl valla vakiyāṟ peṟṟēṉ; eṉiṉum, avaḷi avaṟku uriyaḷākak koṭukkum pēṟu peṟṟavar avaḷāyattōrē yākaliṉ, yāṉ appeyar peṟṟum peṟēṉēyāyiṉēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
attam vaḻi. piṟanilaṅkaḷiṟ pōlātu pāli nilattu vaḻi poḻilkaḷālum piṟavaṟṟālum maṟippuṇṇātu veṟitē neṭittut tōṉṟuvatu paṟṟi, atta nīḷiṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. vallāṟu, īṇṭu iṉpappayaṉ tārāmi cuṭṭi niṉṟatu. maṉṟa: tēṟṟapporuṭṭu. āyattōr eṉṟatu tōḻiyi; itu muṉṉilippuṟamoḻi. “atuveṉ urupukeṭak kukaram varumē 1” eṉṟataṉāl nāṉkaṉurupu virintatu.
“tāyeṉap paṭuvōḷ cevili yākum 2” eṉṟataṉāl, tāyar eṉṉum peyarē eṉṟum, tāyāvāḷ talimakaḷiṉ arumaṟi aṟintu aṟattoṭunili vakiyāl maṇam puṇarttum māṇpiṉaḷākat tāṉ atu peṟāmiyāl varuntu kiṉṟamiyiṉ, vallāṟu eṭuttēṉ eṉṟum, uṇmit tāyarpōl avaḷ maṟiyuṟavu aṟintu avaṉiṭip paṭuttu mēmpāṭu eytiṉōr āyattō rākaliṉ, avarē tāyar eṉappaṭaṟku uriyōreṉpāḷ, koṭuttōr maṉṟa avaḷ āyattōr eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itu poṟāmi yeṉṉum meyppāṭu.
itu cevili teruṭṭuvārkkuk kūṟiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar 3.
39. uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ
iṭiccurattu uritta pattu
itu koṇṭutalik kaḻintavaḻi iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭōrum, tēṭic cellum tāyarum, koṇṭutalik kaḻiyum talimakkaḷumākiya palarum nikaḻttum kūṟṟukkaḷiṉ tokuti yākaliṉ, ippeyarttāyiṟṟu.
	381.	piṅkāy nelli palavuṭaṉ micintu
ceṅkāṉ marāatta variniḻa liruntōr
yārko laḷiyar tāmē vārciṟik
kuṟuṅkāṉ makaṉṟi laṉṉa
uṭaṉpuṇar koḷkik kāta lōrē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ iṭicurattuk kaṇṭār colliyatu.
uri :
nelliyiṉ paciya kāykaḷip palaviṉ kaṉiyōṭu uṇṭu. civanta aṭimarattiyuṭiya ceṅkaṭampu marattiṉ iṭi yiṭṭa niḻaliṉkaṇ mēviyiruntōr, nīṇṭa ciṟakukaḷiyum kuṟukiya kālkaḷiyumuṭiya makaṉṟiṟ paṟavi pōlap pirivaritākiya koḷki mikka kātaliyuṭiya rākaliṉ, ivarkaḷ yāvarkolō, aḷikkattakkōrāy irā niṉṟār eṉṟavāṟu.
paciya kāykaḷiyuṭiya nelliyiṉ kaṉikaḷ palavaṟṟi uṇṭu eṉiṉumām. curattiṭic celvōr nellik kaṉiyi uṇṭal marapu. “cēynāṭṭuc, cuvikkāy nellip pōkkarum poṅkar, vīḻkaṭit tiraḷkāy oruṅkuṭaṉ tiṉṟu 1” eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. variniḻal, ilicceṟiviṉṟi iṭi
yiṭṭu viḷaṅkum niḻal. makaṉṟil, oṉṟi yoṉṟu pirintuḻi uyirvāḻāta pēraṉpuṭiya nīril vāḻum paṟavi vaki. “nīruṟi makaṉṟiṟ puṇarcci pōlap, pirivari tākiya taṇṭāk kāmamoṭu 2” eṉṟār piṟarum.
oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappiṉarāyp puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya koḷkiyiṉiyu muṭiyarāyt tōṉṟutaliṉ, makaṉṟil aṉṉa, uṭaṉpuṇar koḷkik kātalōrē eṉṟum, ivar cellum neṟi vevviya curamum koṭiya vilaṅku vaḻaṅkuvatu mākaliṉ, annalaṉiṉmi karuti, yārkol aḷiyar eṉṟum kūṟiṉār; “poḻutum āṟum uṭkuvarat tōṉṟi, vaḻuvi ṉākiya kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭalum 3” kaṇ ṭōrk kiyalpākaliṉ. “yār kol aḷiyar tāmē....... veypayil aḻuvam muṉṉiyōrē 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa.
	382.	puḷḷolik kamartta kaṇṇaḷ veḷvēl
tiruntukaḻaṟ kāḷiyo ṭaruñcuraṅ kaḻivōḷ
elliṭi yacinta kalleṉ cīṟūrp
puṉiyiḻi makaḷirp payanta
maṉikeḻu peṇṭirkku nōvumār peritē.
itu, talimakaḷ iṭiccurattiṉatu ūriṉkaṇ elliṭit taṅkiyavaḻi avvūrp peṇṭir pārttu iraṅkutal kaṇṭōr colliyatu.
uri :
puḷḷiṉam ceyta oliyāṉ añci maruṇṭa kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyaḷāy, veḷḷiya vēliyum aḻakiya kaḻaliyu muṭiya kāḷipōlvāṉōṭu puṇarntu, cellutaṟ kariya curattiṉkaṇ cellum ivaḷ, iraviṉkaṇ taṅkutalāl kalleṉṉum ōci mikka ic ciṟiya ūrkkaṇṇuḷḷa tiruntiya iḻi yaṇinta makaḷirip payanta perumaṉik kiḻattiyarkku ivaḷik kāṇap peritum varutta muṇṭāva tāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
mūṉṟāvataṉ poruṭkaṇ nāṉkāvatu vantu mayaṅkiṟṟu. amarttal, maruḷutal; “civantoḷi maḻuṅki amarttaṉa kollō 2” eṉpataṉāl uṇarka. acital, taṅkutal. puṉiyiḻi makaḷir, piṟar tuṇiyiṉṟit tāmē iḻiyaṇintu koḷḷum vaḷarcci peṟāta iḷampeṇkaḷ. maṉikeḻu peṇṭir, illuṟiyum nalliyal makaḷir. “nuṉikuḻit talamarum nocci, maṉikeḻu peṇṭiyāṉ ākuka maṉṉē 3” eṉṟār piṟarum. “acinilik kiḷavi yākuvaḻi aṟital 4” eṉṟataṉāl ār acinili.
tām payilātaviṭattuc ceṉṟa kāli, āṇṭup puḷḷiṉam olittālum iḷamakaḷirkku viyappum accamum tōṉṟi maruṭci yuṇṭākku mākaliṉ, puḷḷolikku amartta kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟaṉar; “varippuṟap puṟaviṉ pulampukoḷ teḷ
viḷi, uruppavir amiyattu amarppaṉaḷ nōkki 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. talimakkaḷiṉ uruvum tiruvum oppum uyarvum kaṇṭa ūravarkku viyappum makiḻcciyum mikutaliṉ āravāram eḻuntamipaṟṟik kalleṉ cīṟūr eṉṟaṉar.
ivar oḻukkaṅ kaṇṭa peṟṟōr, tām payanta makaḷir orukāl ataṉi mēṟkoḷva reṉṉum accamum, peṟaṟkariya tāytantiyariyum, taṉ aiyar āyattōr mutaliya piṟariyum pirintu pōkkut tuṇintāḷik kaṇṭu avar āṟṟārātaliṉ, avarit tēṟṟutaṟkaṇṇuḷatākiya muyaṟcittuṉpamum mikku varuntukiṉṟaṉar eṉpār, nōvumār peritē eṉṟār.
	383.	kōṭcurum paraṟṟum nāṭcurat tamaṉṟa
neṭuṅkāṉ marāattuk kuṟuñciṉi paṟṟi
valañcuri vāliṇar koytaṟku niṉṟa
maḷḷa ṉuḷḷam makiḻkūrn taṉṟē
pañcāyp pāvikkun taṉakkum
añcāyk kūnta lāyvatu kaṇṭē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkiya talimakaḷ talimakaṉ vaḷitta kompiṟ pūk koṇṭu taṉakkum pāvikkum vakukkak kaṇṭār kūṟiyatu.
uri :
curattiṉkaṇ, neruṅka vuḷḷa neṭiya aṭippakutiyi yuṭiya marāmarattiṉ, tēṉ koḷḷum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya curumpukaḷ olikkiṉṟa kuṟukiya kiḷiyit tāḻtti, aḻakiya melliya kūntaliyuṭiya taṉ kātali, ataṉkaṇ nāṭ kāliyil valamākac cuḻittu malarnta veḷḷiya pūkkaḷik koytaṟporuṭṭu, celavu ciṟitu tavirntu niṉṟa kāḷi, avaḷ appūkkaḷit taṉakkum taṉ pañcāyppāvikkum eṉa vakuppatu kaṇṭu makiḻcci mikukiṉṟatu, kāṇmiṉ eṉṟa
vāṟu.
kōḷ, koḷḷutal. amalutal, neruṅkutal. marāam, īṇṭu veṇkaṭampu. itaṉ pūvitaḻ valamākac cuḻittiruppatu paṟṟi valañcuri vāliṇar eṉṟār. veṇṇiṟappū eṉṟaṟku vāliṇar eṉappaṭṭatu. “valañcuri marāattuc curaṅkamaḻ 
putuvī 1” eṉṟum, “vāliya cuti virintaṉṉa palpū marāam 2” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇka. kuṟuñciṉi paṟṟik koy taṟkuc ciṉiyit tāḻtti niṉṟa maḷḷaṉ eṉa iyippiṉum amiyum. pañcāyppāvi, kōriyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvi. cāyal, meṉmi. añcāyk kūntal eṉak koṇṭu, aimpālākiya aḻakiya kūntal eṉṟumām. mañcāy kūntal eṉak koṇṭu, mañcu pōl aḻakiya kūntal eṉṟalu moṉṟu. kūntaliyuṭiya kātali ciṉipaṟṟik koytaṟku niṉṟa maḷḷaṉ uḷḷam kaṇṭu makiḻ kūrntaṉṟu eṉa muṭikka.
kaṭampiṉ naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pūvik kaṇṭāṭku ataṉi aṇintukōṭaṟku viruppam cēṟaliṉ tāḻa niṉṟa kuṟuñ ciṉiyip paṟṟiṉāḷāka, avaḷvēṭki kaṇṭa maḷḷaṉ taṉ celavutavirntāṉ eṉpār, kuṟuñciṉi paṟṟik koytaṟku niṉṟa eṉṟār. piṟāṇṭum, “niṉ pomma lōti potuḷa vāri, arumpaṟa malarnta āypū marāattuc, curumpucūḻ alari vēyntaniṉ, tēmpāy kūntal kuṟumpala mocikkum vaṇṭu kaṭin tōmpal tēṟṟāy..... paṇittōḷ viḷaṅka vīci valluvi maṉṉāl naṭiyē 3” eṉat talivaṉ urippatu kāṇka.
tammāṟ kātalikkap paṭṭār ceyal kāṇuntōṟum kātalarkku uḷḷam makiḻciṟattal iyalpākaliṉ āyvatu kaṇṭu, maḷḷaṉ uḷḷam makiḻkūrn taṉṟē eṉṟār.
	384.	cēṭpula muṉṉiya vacinaṭi yantaṇir
nummoṉ ṟirantaṉeṉ moḻiva lemmūr
tāynayan teṭutta vāynalaṅ kaviṉa
āriṭi yiṟantaṉa ḷeṉmiṉ
nēriṟi muṉkiyeṉ ṉāyat tōrkkē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkiya talimakaḷ āṇṭu etirvarum anta ṇarkkuc colliyatu.
uri :
cēyttāya nilatti eṇṇi acinta naṭiyiṉiyuṭi yirāyc cellukiṉṟa antaṇarkaḷē, uṅkaḷiyoṉṟu irantu kūṟukiṟēṉ; emmūrkkuc celliṉ, nēriya iṟi poruntiya muṉkiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉtōḻiyarkku “nuṅkaḷ tōḻiyāvāḷ taṉ tāy pēṇi vaḷartta aḻakiya nalam vaṉappumika, cellutaṟ kariya curaṅkaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉaḷ” eṉak kūṟuvīrkaḷāka eṉṟavāṟu.
eññāṉṟum pukaḻum pūciyum peṟutaṟ kuriya nāṭu nōkkic cellum iyalpiṉarāyum, ūrti yiṉṟi naṭantu cellum paṭivattarāyum iruttaliṉ, cēṭpulam muṉṉiya vacinaṭi yantaṇir eṉṟār. antaṇar piṟapulam cēṟum iyalpiṉarātal, “vevviṭic celaṉ māli yoḻukkattīr 1” eṉṟum, naṭantu cellum paṭivattarātal, āciriyar marapiyalil ēṉi yaracarkku “naṭi navil puraviyum kaḷiṟum tērum 2” kūṟiyatu pōla, ūrti kūṟāmiyālum, “kuṭiyārniḻal uṟicēr karakattoṭu kuṉṟiṭattu, naṭiyāṉ melintu varukiṉṟa antaṇir 3” eṉṟu kūṟutalālum aṟika.
makaḷatu nalam pēṇutalilēyē tāyiṉ kaṇṇum karuttum oṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ, taṉ nalattit tāy nayanteṭutta āynalam eṉṟāḷ. “āyamum ayalum maruḷat tāyōm, pāynalam4” eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
iḵtu, “eñciyōrkkum eñcutal ilavē5” eṉṟataṉāl, talivi kūṟṟāyiṟṟu. antaṇar cāṉṟōr aruntavattōr mutalāyiṉārip paṇintu kūṟal vēṇṭum eṉpatu kaṟpuneṟi yātaliṉ, irantaṉeṉ moḻival eṉṟum, eññāṉṟum taṉnalamē kuṟikkoṇṭu pēṇiya ciṟappuṭimi karutiyum, talimakaṉoṭu kūṭātavaḻi nalaṅkuṉṟum eṉa añcip pōk kuṭaṉ paṭṭut tuṇiceytaṉar ākaliṉālum, tāy nayan teṭutta vāynalam eṉṟum, nēriṟi muṉki eṉ āyat tōrkku eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
talimakaṉ talimakaṭku oḻukkaṅ kaṟpittal, “antaṇar tiṟattuñ cāṉṟōr tēettum, antamil ciṟappiṟ piṟarpiṟar tiṟattiṉum, oḻukkaṅ kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉum 1” eṉpataṉāl uṇarka. tāy nayanteṭutta nala meṉpuḻi uṭimiyum, āyattōrkku eṉpuḻi aruḷalum, irantaṉeṉ eṉpuḻit taṉmiyumākiya meyppāṭukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
iḵtu yāṉ pōkiṉṟatu āyattiṟku urimiṉ eṉṟatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 2 iḵtu iṭic curattuc celkiṉ ṟāḷ taṉ pati celvārik kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. 3
	385.	kaṭuṅkaṭ kāḷiyoṭu neṭuntē rēṟik
kōḷval vēṅki malipiṟak koḻiya
vēṟupal laruñcura miṟantaṉa ḷavaḷeṉak
kūṟumiṉ vāḻiyō vāṟuceṉ mākkaḷ
naṟṟō ṇayantupā rāṭṭi
eṟkeṭut tirunta vaṟaṉil yāykkē.
itu, varivu maṟuttuḻi uṭaṉpōya talimakaḷ iṭic curattuk kaṇṭāri, “yāṉ pōkiṉṟapaṭiyi yāykku nīr kūṟavēṇṭum” eṉac colliyatu.
uri :
neṟiyiṟ cellum māntarkaḷē, eṉṉuṭiya nalla tōḷkaḷi virumpip pārāṭṭi vaḷarttu, yāṉ tali makaṉōṭu kūṭi eṉ nalattik kaviṉuṟuvittuk koḷḷā vaki iṟceṟittu vitta aṟaviyalpillāta eṉ tāyik kāṇiṉ, avaṭkut taṟukaṇmiyuṭiya kāḷiyākiya taṉ kātalaṉōṭu neṭiya tērmītēṟik, koli valla vēṅki kaḷ mikka malikaḷ piṟpaṭa, muṉṉāka uḷḷa palavāy vēṟupaṭṭa cellutaṟ kariya curaṅkaḷik kaṭantu niṉmakaḷ ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉa urippīrkaḷāka eṉṟavāṟu.
kaṭuṅkaṇ, taṟukaṇ. pakimi karutit tīmiceyvārik kaṇṇōṭātu oṟuttaṟ kuriya taṟukaṇ kaṭuṅkaṇ eṉavum, aruḷ karuti aṭipaṇivārkku utavum taṟukaṇ meṉkaṇ eṉavumām. itaṉic cērntōr puṉkaṇ añcum paṇpiṉ meṉkaṇ 1” eṉpar nalvēṭṭaṉār; ūrāṇmi eṉpar tiruvaḷḷuvar. 2 neṭuntolivu cellutaṟ kuriya vaṉmi yuṭiyatu eṉṟaṟku neṭuntēr eṉṟār. kāl valiya tēr eṉ patu karuttu 3. kōḷ, koli. mākkaḷ, aṇmiviḷi. keṭut tirunta yāy, kāṇāmiyāl varuntum tāy eṉṟu kūṟiṉu mām; “keṭuttut toṭu naṉkalam eṭuttukkoṇ ṭāṅku 4” eṉṟāṟ pōla.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭut talivaṉōṭu cellum talivi, cellum neṟiyiṭit taṉakku ētam uḷavām eṉa eṇṇi varuntum tāykkut taṉṉōṭu pōtarum kātalaṉ tiṟali, kaṭuṅkaṇ kāḷi eṉṟum, naṭantu cēṟaliṉṟi valiya tērūrntu cellumāṟu viḷaṅka, neṭuntēr ēṟi eṉṟum, tīṅku ceyyum vilaṅkukaḷ uṟiyum malinilattiyum piṟa nilaṅkaḷiyum iṉitu kaṭantu ceṉṟu cērnta tiṟam tōṉṟa, kōḷval vēṅki malipiṟak koḻiya eṉṟum, aruñcuram iṟan taṉaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
kuḻavipparuvam toṭaṅki avaḷ nalamē pēṇi vaḷarttaṉa ḷākaliṉ, naṟṟōḷ nayantu pārāṭṭi eṉṟum, talimaka ṉōṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukiyaviṭattu avaḷmēṉi vēṟupāṭum alareḻuntamiyum kaṇṭu iṟceṟittaṉaḷ eṉiṉum, acceyal tāṉ talivaṉōṭu talippeyaṟkut taṭiyāyiṉamiyiṉ eṟkeṭuttu irunta eṉṟum, piṟarkku nōy cūḻvār tāyarāyiṉum tīyarē yākaliṉ, aṟaṉil yāy eṉṟum, tāṉ talimakaṉiṭam taliyaḷi peṟutaliṉiṉṟu takintaṉa ḷāyiṉum, taṉ naṟṟōḷi nayantu pārāṭṭiya naṉṟi karutit taṉṉik kuṟittu avaḷ tēṭi varuntāvakik kūṟumiṉ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. tāṉ vēṇṭuva toṉṟaṉi vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceytal paṟṟi vāḻiyō eṉṟāḷ.
naṟṟōḷ nayantu pārāṭṭiya tāy taṉṉi iṟceṟittu ōmpiyatu ūravar kūṟum alar añciyum taṉ nalaṅkarutiyumē eṉpatu niṉintu, atu tāymiyiṉ aṟam eṉṟu uṇarntaṉa ḷāyiṉum, talimakaṟku varivu maṟutta koṭumiyi nōkka vēṟātalāl, veṟutta uḷḷatta ḷātaliṉ, taṉ tāyi aṟaṉil yāy eṉṟāḷ eṉavumām. “vaḷḷikīḻ vīḻā varimicit tēṉtoṭā, kolli kuralvāṅki īṉā malivāḻnar, alla purintoḻuka lāṉ 1” eṉat talivi tamar varivu maṟuttamikku varuntik kūṟumāṟu kāṇka.
iṉṟu yāṉ tērēṟi varuttamiṉṟip pōkiṉṟami  yāykku urimiṉ eṉṟatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 2 iḵtu iṭic curattu āyattārkkuc colliviṭṭatu eṉpar iḷam pūraṇar. 3 uṭaṉpōkkil tērēṟippōtal uṇṭeṉpataṟku itaṉik kāṭṭuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 4 curattiṭiyavaḷē eṉṟum pāṭam.
	386.	puṉkaṇ yāṉiyoṭu pulivaḻaṅ kattam
nayanta kātalaṟ puṇarntuceṉ ṟaṉaḷē
neṭuñcuvar nalliṉ maruṇṭa
iṭumpi yuṟuviniṉ kaṭuñcūṉ makaḷē.
itu, puṇarntuṭaṉpōkiya talimakaḷi iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭār avaḷ tāykkuc ceṉṟu kūṟiyatu.
uri :
neṭiya cuvar cūḻnta nalla illiṉkaṇ mayaṅki oṭuṅkutaṟ kētuvākiya tuṉpam uṟupavaḷē, niṉ mutaṟcūlil tōṉ ṟiya makaḷākiya talimakaḷ, taṉṉi virumpiya kātala ṉuṭaṉ kūṭic ciṟiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷum pulikaḷum iyaṅkukiṉṟa vaḻikaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉaḷ; ākaliṉ, nī varuntutal oḻika eṉṟavāṟu.
puṉkaṇ eṉṟa viṭattup puṉmi, ciṟumi mēṟṟu; puṉ kaṇ eṉṟē koṇṭu vēṉil vemmiyāl nīrum niḻaliṭamum peṟātu eytum miṭimi yeṉṟu urippiṉum amiyum; “poruḷvēṇṭum puṉkaṇmi īṇṭilli 1” eṉṟāṟpōla. ōṭu: eṇṇupporuṭṭu. pakaṟpōtil yāṉiyum iravil puliyum vaḻaṅkutaliṉ iraṇṭiyum cērak kūṟiṉār, ep pōtum cēṟal arumi pulappaṭuttaṟku. neṭuñcuvar nallil eṉṟaviṭattu neṭuñcuvar kāvalarumiyum naṉmi celva muṭimiyum kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. maruḷutal, īṇṭuk kiyaṟṟu varuntal. iṭumpi makaṭ pirivi āṟṟāmiyāṟ piṟanta tuṉpam. iṭumpiyuṟuvi, iṭumpi yeyti varuntum tāy. kaṭuñcūl taliccūl; “kaṭuñcūṟ ciṟuvaṉ 2” eṉṟami kāṇka.
niṉ maṉipperumiyum celvanaṉmiyum niṉakku iṉpam taruvatu pōla, niṉ makaṭkum tarum eṉak karuti maruḷukiṉṟaṉi; tāṉum niṉṉip pōl maṉivāḻvu ceyyum māṇpuṭi neñcattāl ivaṟṟi uḷḷātu kaḻintāḷ eṉpatit teru ḷātu varuntutal vēṇṭā eṉpār, neṭuñcuvar nallil maruṇṭaṉi eṉṟum, maruṭci kāraṇamāka nī tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉi eṉṟaṟku maruṇṭa iṭumpi uṟuvi eṉṟum, kaṟpuṭip perumaṭantiyāyiṉum, talippaṭu kātaṟ kāmappayaṉākap peṟṟa taliccūl makaḷātal paṟṟi niṉakku immaruṭci kim mikku varuttukiṟatu eṉpār niṉ kaṭuñcūl makaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. iṉi, avaḷ ceṉṟa neṟiyiṉ koṭumiyiṉi eṇṇik kalaṅkukiṉṟāyāyiṉ, yām avvaḻiyiṉiṉṟu varu kiṉṟēm; avaḷ ceṉṟavaḻi eppōtum makkaḷ vaḻaṅkutaṟ kākāta koṭumiyuṭittāyiṉum, ataṉiyum avaḷ kaṭantu ceṉṟoḻintāḷ eṉṟaṟku, puṉkaṇ yāṉiyoṭu puli vaḻaṅku attam ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṟum. avaḷum avaḷ kātala ṉum oruvari yoruvar iṉṟi amiyāk kātalāṟ piṇip puṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpār nayanta kātalaṟ puṇarntu ceṉṟa ṉaḷ eṉṟum viḷakkiṉar. kātalaṉatu kātaliṉpattil makaḷ kaḷittuc cella, makaṭkut tāy maruṇṭa maṉattoṭu maṉiyiṉ kaṇ iṭumpiyuṟṟu iṉital iṉṉātu eṉat teruṭṭiyavāṟu.
itu, “tāynili kaṇṭu taṭuppiṉum viṭuppiṉum 1” eṉṟataṉāl, taṭuttalum, neṟiyiṉ koṭumi kūṟip puṇarn tōr pāṅkiṟ puṇarnta neñcamoṭu aḻintetir kūṟi viṭuttalumām.
	387.	aṟampuri yarumaṟi naviṉṟa nāviṉ
tiṟampuri koḷki yantaṇir toḻuvaleṉ
ṟoṇṭoṭi viṉavum pētiyam peṇṭē
kaṇṭeṉa mamma curattiṭi yavaḷē
iṉṟuṇi yiṉitu pārāṭṭak
kuṉṟuyar piṟaṅkaṉ maliyiṟan tōḷē.
itu, piṉceṉṟa ceviliyāl viṉāvappaṭṭa antaṇar avaṭkuc colliyatu.
uri :
aṟattic collukiṉṟa ariya maṟikaḷi ōtip payiṉṟa nāviṉiyum atu vitikkum ceyalvaki teṟin tāṟṟum oḻukkattiyu muṭiya antaṇarkaḷē, toḻu kiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu colli, oḷḷiya toṭi yaṇinta eṉ  makaḷ ceṉṟāḷik kaṇṭīrō eṉa viṉavum pētimi yuṭiya maṉikeḻu peṇṭē, yām avaḷic curattiṭiyē kaṇṭēm. iṉṉum kēṭpāyāka: iṉiya tuṇivaṉāṉa kātalaṉ makiḻntu nalam pārāṭṭi varak kuṉṟukaḷ ceṟinta uyarnta malikaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟāḷ; ākalāṉ, nī avaḷip piṉceṉṟu varunta vēṇṭā, mīḷka eṉṟavāṟu.
aṟamāvatu, viyattu vāḻvāṅku vāḻntu uyarnta aṟivuṭi naṉmakkaḷ, vāḻkkikku uṟutiyum naṉmiyum payappaṉa iviyeṉa aṟintu, vitittum vilakkiyum urikkum naṉmoḻi. anneṟiyil, makkaṭku uṟuti eṉa avarkaḷ kaṇṭavi aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vīṭu, ivvulakiṟ peṟappaṭuva taṉṟu; ivvulaka vāḻkkikku maṉṉiyavi ēṉi mūṉṟumē. am mūṉṟaṉuḷ aritākiya aṟatti virumpi urippatu paṟṟi maṟi aṟampuri arumaṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. ulakil vāḻum uyirpporuḷ uyiril poruḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum tōṟṟamum muṭivum iv vulakamē aṉṟi itaṉ mēl vēṟoṉṟu milli eṉa ellā rālum eḷitil urikkappaṭum karutti maṟuttu, uyir poru ḷiṉ vēṟāy uyarnta pēruyirākiya iṟivaṉ uṇṭeṉṟum, ivvulaka uyirp poruṭkellām vēṟāyt tuṉpamē illāta iṉpa aruḷulaku oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttuvatu paṟṟi anta aṟanūl maṟi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu.
ēṉip poruḷiṉpa nukarccikaṭku neṟikāṭṭi vīṭu pēṟākiya iṟiyaruḷ iṉpavulakavāḻvukku urimi ceyyum arumi paṟṟi aṟanūlākiya maṟiyit tamiḻc cāṉṟōr aṟampuri arumaṟi eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar. “nāṉmaṟik kuṟittaṉṟu aruḷā kāmiyiṉ, aṟaṅkuṟit taṉṟu poruḷā kutaliṉ 1” eṉṟum, “eḻutāk kaṟpiṉ niṉcol luḷḷum, pirintōrp puṇarkkum paṇpiṉ, maruntum uṇṭō 2” eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟutaliṉ, arumaṟikkaṇ poruḷum iṉpamum kūṟappaṭāmi teḷiyappaṭum. iḵtu eḻutappaṭāmal oruvar piṉ oruvarāka nāvāl navilappaṭṭu vantamiyiṉ, maṟavāmip poruṭṭu eppōtum navilvatu maṟiyōr marapu. atu paṟṟiyē, arumaṟi naviṉṟa nāviṉ antaṇar eṉṟār. tiṟam, aṟattiṉkūṟu. maṉiyiṉkaṇ vāḻvāṅku vāḻntu aṟam kāṭṭum vīṭupēṟu kuṟittut tuṟantōrum, iḷamiyilēyē aṟappayaṉ viḻintu tuṟantōrum eṉa aṟavōr iruvakiya rātaliṉ, iru tiṟattōrkkum ōraṉṉa oḻukkam poruntāmi yāṉ, aṟattuṟiyil tiṟampala tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ, avaṟṟit tiṟam eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ tam takutikkum cālpukkum ēṟṟatu mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukum koḷki tiṟampuri koḷkiyām eṉṟum aṟika. koḷki, oḻukkam. “kulañcuṭum koḷki piḻippiṉ 3” eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟuvatu kāṇka. aṟavōr eṉṉātu antaṇar eṉṟār, “evvuyirkkum centaṇmi pūṇṭu oḻukutalāṉ.4” antaṇar eṉpatu antaṇir eṉa viḷiyēṟṟatu; “aruvum ārum īroṭu civaṇum 5” eṉpatu viḷi marapu. oṇṭoṭi īṇṭut taliviyik kuṟittatu. amma: kēṭpāyāka eṉa varum iṭiccol. colli eṉpatu colleccam. peṇṭu, peṇṇeṉṉum poruḷpaṭa vaḻaṅkum potuccol. iṉitu, nalam.
piṟaṅkal, īṇṭuc ceṟital. oṇṭoṭi viṉavum peṇṭē, curattiṭik kaṇṭaṉem; avaḷ, iṉṟuṇi pārāṭṭa mali iṟantāḷ eṉa iyiyum. ākalāṉ eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟip peccam.
makaḷ niliyi uḷḷavāṟu aṟiyum viruppiṉaḷātaliṉ, talimakaḷit tēṭip piṉceṉṟa cevili, niṉivilum nāvilum aṟam nilavum koḷkiyiṉarāṉa antaṇarkaḷi, aṟampuri arumaṟi naviṉṟa nāviṉ, tiṟampuri koḷki antaṇir eṉṟāḷ. taṉ tuṉpanili kaṇṭu iraṅki vēṟu aṟaṅkaḷic collik kaḻiyātu kaṇṭatu kaṇṭāṅkuk kūṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟaṟku avaratu nāviṉic ciṟappittāḷ eṉa aṟika. viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi ellāriyum oppak karuti uṟuti kūṟum oḻukkat tava rātalāl, taṉmakaḷik kaṇṭiruppar eṉṟu eṇṇiṉā ḷātaliṉ, tiṟampuri koḷki antaṇir eṉṟu viḷampiṉāḷ. antaṇari ōmpal ilvāḻvōrkkuk kaṭaṉātal paṟṟit toḻutal koṇṭukūṟappaṭṭatu. toḻuval eṉṟatu mukamaṉ. oṇṭoṭiyākiya talimakaḷ taṉ kātalaṉuṭaṉ koṇṭu talikkaḻintatu kūṟi avaḷik kaṇṭīrō eṉa viṉaviṉāṭku viṭiyiṟukkiṉṟā rākaliṉ, avaḷ kūṟṟiṉiyē koṇ ṭeṭuttu, oṇṭoṭi viṉavum peṇṭē eṉṟum, koṇṭu talikkaḻital kaṟpuk kākkum poṟpuṭiya aṟamātali aṟiyātu ētam koṇṭu varuntumāṟu tōṉṟap pētiyam peṇṭē eṉṟum, aḵtu avaḷ viṉāvukkuc cevvaṉiṟi yākāmiyiṉ, kaṇṭaṉem curattiṭi eṉṟum, atu kēṭṭa tum uḷam makiḻntu avaḷatu nalamaṟiya eḻunta vēṭkiyi mukakkuṟippāl uṇarntu mēlum kūṟaluṟutaliṉ amma eṉṟum, avaḷ, taṉakkuriya uyirttuṇivaṉōṭē celkiṉṟā ḷeṉ pār, iṉṟuṇi eṉṟum, vaḻiyiṉatu arumi tōṉṟāvāṟu kātalaṉ avaḷatu nalam pārāṭṭic celvatāl makiḻcci mikkuc celkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟaṟku iṉitu pārāṭṭa eṉṟum, ataṉāl neṟiyiṭi niṉṟa kuṉṟum maliyum avaṭku ayarcci viḷiviyā vāka, avaḷ curatti iṉitu kaṭantupōṉāḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkak kuṉṟuyar piṟaṅkal maliyiṟantōḷē eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. “ciṟantāṉi vaḻipaṭīic ceṉṟaṉaḷ, aṟantali piriyā āṟumaṟṟu atuvē 1” eṉac cāṉṟōr koṇṭutalik kaḻitali aṟam eṉpatum, “vāṉtaḷi poḻinta kāṇpiṉ kāli, aṇikiḷar kalāvam aituvirit tiyalum, maṇipuri yeruttiṉ maññi pōlaniṉ, vīpey kūntal vīcuvaḷi yuḷara, ēkuti maṭanti 2” eṉa iṉṟuṇi iṉitu pārāṭṭuvatum,  “kaṇṭiciṉ vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ nunti, aṭukaḷam pāynta toṭiciti maruppiṉ, piṭimiṭi kaḷiṟṟiṉ tōṉṟum, kuṟu neṭun tuṇiya kuṉṟamum uṭittē 3” eṉak kuṉṟuyar piṟaṅkal mali kāṭṭit talivaṉ ayarcci pōkkuvatum kāṇka.
itu cevili viṉāyavaḻik kūṟiyatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar 4 ; varaviṉkaṭ kūṟiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 5.
	388.	neruppavir kaṉali yuruppucciṉan taṇiyak
karuṅkāl yāattu variniḻa laciic
ciṟuvari yiṟappiṟ kāṇkuvi ceṟitoṭip
poṉṉēr mēṉi maṭantiyoṭu
veṉvēl viṭali muṉṉiya curaṉē.
itu, tēṭic ceṉṟa cevilikku iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭār avaḷik kaṇṭa tiṟam kūṟiyatu.
uri :
neruṅkiya toṭiyiṉiyum poṉpōlum aḻakiya mēṉiyiṉiyumuṭiya maṭantiyuṭaṉ veṉṟi tarum vēlēn tiya viṭaliyāvāṉ muṉṉic ceṉṟa curatti, neruppu viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppam taṇiyumaḷavum kariya aṭi yiṉiyuṭiya yāmarattiṉ ceṟiyāta niḻaliṉkaṇ taṅki, ciṟumalikaḷik kaṭantu celliṉ kāṇpāy eṉṟavāṟu.
ñāyiṟṟiṉ mikka oḷi neruppāyt tākkutaliṉ nerup pavir kaṉali eṉṟār. kaṉali, ñāyiṟu. uruppu, vemmi. “neruppeṉac civanta uruppavir maṇṭilam 1” eṉpar piṟarum. vemmiyic ciṉameṉṟatu ilakkaṇi. yā marattiṉ aṭi karuminiṟa muṭittātaliṉ, karuṅkāl yā eṉṟār; “kavi yoṇ taḷira karuṅkāl yāattu, vēṉil veṟpu 2” eṉpatu kāṇka. acital, taṅkutal, “olkunilik kaṭukki yalkuniḻal acii 3” eṉṟāṟ pōla. oṭu: oruviṉiyoṭu.
talimakaḷip piṉtoṭarntu curattiṭi naṭantu melintu varum ceviliyiṉ tuyarnili kaṇṭu kaḻivirakka muṟṟu etirē kiṭanta curattiṉ vemmiyi āṟṟāḷeṉpatu paṟṟi, āṇṭut tōṉṟiya yāmarattik kāṭṭi, ataṉ nīḻalil veyilveppam taṇiyumaḷavum taṅkumāṟu paṇikkiṉṟamiyiṉ, neruppavir kaṉali uruppuc ciṉam taṇiyak karuṅkāl yāttu variniḻal acii eṉṟum, neṭituyarntu kiḷiparappi niḻalceytu niṟkum nīrmi nōkki, karuṅkāl yāttu variniḻal eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. itaṉāl puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya niṉ makaḷum veyil vemmi nōkki yāniḻal taṅkic ceṉṟaṉar eṉak kuṟitta vāṟāyiṟṟu. ciṟuvari eṉṟār, malivu tōṉṟāmip poruṭṭu. acii iṟappiṉ kāṇkuvi eṉṟatu, taṅkātu celkuvi yāyiṉ, āṟṟātu variyi iṟattalum kāṇṭalum illiyām eṉṟavāṟu. toṭiyiṉ ceṟivum mēṉiyiṉ polivum eṭuttōtiṉar, kātalaṉ puṇarntuṭaṉ varutalāl vaḻinaṭit taḷarcciyiṉmiyumuḷḷakkiḷarcciyuṭimiyum talivipāl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ. maṭantiyi muṉ ṉuyttut talimakaṉ avaḷpiṉ cēṟaliṉ, maṭanti yoṭu eṉṟār. “niṉ aṇimāṇ ciṟupuṟam kāṇkam ciṟunaṉi, ēkeṉa ēkal nāṇi niṉṟu, taliyiṟiñci yōḷē atukaṇṭu, yāmmun tuṟutal cellēm 4” eṉat talimakaṉ urippataṉāl, uṭaṉpōkkil taliviyi muṉṉuyttuc cēṟal iyalpātal kāṇka.
curaṉ kāṇkuvi eṉṟatu, curattiṭi avarkaḷik kāṇalām eṉṟaṟku, itu cevilikku iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭōr numakku eytac cēṟalām eṉṟu viṭuttatu eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar 1.
	389.	ceyviṉip polinta ceṟikaḻa ṉōṉṟāḷ miyaṇaṟ kāḷiyoṭu piya viyalip
pāvi yaṉṉaveṉ ṉāytoṭi maṭanti
ceṉṟaṉa ḷeṉṟi riya
oṉṟiṉa vōvava ḷañcilam paṭiyē.
itu, piṉceṉṟa cevilittāy viṉavappaṭṭōr 'kaṇṭōm’ eṉpuḻic colliyatu.
uri :
aiya, eṉ kaṇṇiṟ pāvipōlum āyntu koṇṭa toṭiyiṉi yaṇinta eṉmakaḷ, toḻilvakiyāṟ poli vuṟac ceyyappaṭṭa ceṟinta kaḻaliṉiyum valiya tāḷiṉiyum kariya tāṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya kāḷipōlvāṉōṭu mella naṭantu ceṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟīr; atupōtu ava ḷuṭiya aḻakiya cilampaṇinta aṭikaḷ nilattiṟ poruntiṉa kollō? eṉṟavāṟu.
makaḷirkkuc cilampupōla āṭavarkkuk kālil kaḻal aṇital paṇṭiyōr iyalpu. pūttoḻilum arumpu pōlum vēlippāṭum uṭimipaṟṟik kaḻalic ceyviṉip polinta ceṟikaḻal eṉṟār. āṇmakkaṭku iḷamiyiṟ kiṇkiṇiyum, kāḷipparuvattil kaḻalum aṇital vaḻakku; “kiṇkiṇi kaḷinta kāl oṇkaḻal toṭṭu 2” eṉpatu kāṇka. mikka vēlippāṭuṭimi paṟṟip puṉikaḻal 3 eṉpa; “kaḻal paṟintaṉa 4” eṉṟaṟku “vīrakkaḻal arupputtoḻil paṟintaṉa” eṉa urikkappaṭuvatu itaṟkuc cāṉṟupakarum. ikkaḻal poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭutaliṉ “polaṅkaḻal 5” eṉappaṭum. pakivari veṉṟu avar muṭippoṉṉāl kaḻal ceytu pōrmaṉṉar aṇintu koḷpa. “muṭipuṉinta pacumpoṉṉiṉ, aṭi poliyak kaḻal tiiya, vallāḷaṉi 1” eṉpataṉālaṟika. kaḻal, vīramum veṟṟiyum pukaḻum kuṟittatu; “cuḻalum icivēṇṭi vēṇṭā uyirār kaḻalyāppuk kāriki nīrttu 2” eṉpar tiruvaḷḷuvar. miyaṇal, kariya tāṭimayir. āṇmikkuriya cevvi eytiṉami viḷaṅka miyaṇalik kūṟiṉār. pāvi, kaṇṇiṟpāvi. ōkāram: viṉāpporuṭṭu.
talimakaḷik koṇṭutalikkaḻinta talivaṉuṭiya kaṭṭāṇmiyi viyantu kaṇṭōr cevilipāṟ kūṟiṉa rākaliṉ, avar kūṟṟik koṇṭeṭuttu moḻivāḷ, ceyviṉip polinta ceṟikaḻal nōṉṟāḷ miyaṇaṟ kāḷi eṉṟum, paral niṟinta neṟiyil naṭantu cēṟal tali makaṭku iyalāteṉa niṉintu varuntukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, piya iyali eṉṟum, taṉatu ārākkātal pulappaṭak kaṇṇiṉ pāvi yaṉṉa eṉ āytoṭi maṭanti eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “uṇkaṇ, maṇivāḻ pāvi naṭikaṟ ṟaṉṉeṉ, aṇiyiyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ 3” eṉac cevili kūṟutal kāṇka. kaṇṭōr, tali makaṉ kaḻaliyum tāḷiyum ciṟappittatupōlat taliviyiṉ toṭiyik kūṟic cilampaṇinta aṭiyic ciṟappittuk kūṟāmi yiṉ, avaḷ aṭi naṭiyāl melintu tēyntaṉavō eṉa añci alamarukiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, oṉṟiṉavō avaḷ añcilam paṭiyē eṉṟāḷ.
itu cevili curattiṭi viṉāyatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar 4.
	390.	nallō rāṅkaṭ parantuki toḻutu
pallūḻ maṟuki viṉavu vōyē
tiṇṭōḷ valviṟ kāḷiyoṭu
kaṇṭaṉe maṉṟa curattiṭi yāmē.
itu, piṉceṉṟa cevilittāy palariyum viṉāvak kaṇṭōr tām kaṇṭavāṟu avaṭkuk kūṟiyatu.
uri :
aṟivarit tēṭit tirintu avarpāṟ ki toḻutu pala
muṟiyum maruṇṭu viṉavutaliyuṭiyōy, tiṇṇiya tōḷ kaḷiyum valiya villiṉiyu muṭiya kāḷi pōlvāṉōṭu niṉmakaḷ cella yām iṭiccurattiṟ kaṇṭōm eṉṟavāṟu.
naṟpaṇpu naṟceykikaḷāl uyarnta aṟivarkaḷi nallōr eṉṟār; nallataṉ nalam kāṭṭi ataṉāl ulaku iṉpuṟak kaṇṭu ataṉiyē kātalit toḻukuvatu aṟivar toḻil; aṉṉōr ulakil aruki yiruttaliṉ, avarit tēṭit tirintallatu kāṇpatu arumiyākaliṉ nallōr āṅkaṇ parantu eṉṟār. parattal, tēṭit tirital mēṟṟu, “parantu keṭuka ulakiyaṟṟiyāṉ 1” eṉṟāṟpōla. maṟukutal, tuyarmikutiyāl aṟivu kalaṅkutal. maṉṟa: tēṟṟap poruṭṭāya iṭiccol. yām eṉṟatu, nēriṟ kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭami vaṟpuṟutti niṉṟatu.
nallōrpāṟ celvār toḻutukāṇṭal muṟiyākaliṉ parantu kitoḻutu eṉṟum, arumaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōṉṟu peṟalarum makaḷip pirintu alamarukiṉ ṟāḷākaliṉ, maṟuki eṉṟum, oruvar iruvarōṭu amiyātu palarik kaṇṭu paṉmuṟiyum kēṭpatupaṟṟi, pallūḻ viṉavuvōy eṉṟum appeṟṟiyāḷi mēlum maṟukāvaṇṇam teḷittal teḷḷi yōr kaṭaṉākaliṉ, kaṇṭaṉem maṉṟa eṉṟum, kaṇṭa iṭam itu eṉpār curattiṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
40. maṟutaravup pattu.
itu puṇarntuṭaṉpōkiya talimakkaḷ mīṇṭu varutal poruḷākat toṭukkappaṭṭa pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ toki yākaliṉ, ippeyarttāyiṟṟu. maṟutaravu, mīḷuvatu.
	391. maṟuvi ṟūvic ciṟukaruṅ kākki aṉpuṭi marapiṉiṉ kiḷiyō ṭārap paccūṉ peyta pinniṇa valci
polampuṉi kalattiṟ ṟarukuveṉ mātō
veñciṉaṉ viṟalvēl viṭaliyoṭu
añci lōtiyi varakkarin tīmē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkiya talimakaḷ mīṭaṟporuṭṭut tāy kākattiṟkup parāykkaṭaṉ urittatu.
uri :
kuṟṟamillāta ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya ciṟiya karuṅkākkiyē, vevviya ciṉamum veṟṟiyu muṭiya vēliyēntum kāḷi pōlvāṉoṭu ceṉṟa aḻakiya cilavākiya kūntali yuṭiya eṉ makaḷi varumāṟu karivāyāka. kariyiṉ, aṉpuṭiya niṉ muṟimik kēṟpa niṉ kiḷikkākki kaḷōṭu iruntu nī uṇṇum vakiyil paciya ūṉkalantu ākkiya pacumiyāṉa paliyuṇaviṉip poṉṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa kalattiliṭṭut tarukuvēṉkāṇ. eṉṟavāṟu.
ciṟukaruṅkākki, ciṟumi vaṭivum karumi niṟamum kuṟittaṉa. iḵtu, iṉaccuṭṭillāp paṇpukoḷ peyar. aṉpuṭi marapu, aṉpu kāraṇamāka nilavum muṟimi. kiḷiyō ṭuṇṭal kākkikku marapu; iṉattiṉpāl uḷḷa aṉpāl uḷatāya marapātaliṉ, aṉpuṭi marapu eṉṟār. “kākki karavā karintuṇṇum 1” eṉpatu kāṇka. paciyavūṉ paccūṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. valci, uṇavu. viṭaliyoṭu: oṭu uyar piṉ vaḻittāya oruviṉi oṭu. karintīmē eṉpatu, “muyaṅ kiṉi ceṉṟīmē 2” eṉṟāṟ pōla muṉṉili muṟṟu viṉit tiricol.
ēṉip puṭkaḷippōlac ciṟakariṉ puṟattē palvaki niṟaṅkaḷ viravap peṟāmiyiṉ, maṟuvil tūvic ciṟukaruṅ kākki eṉṟum, peṟṟatu iṟappavum ciṟitāya kālattum taṉ cuṟṟam cūḻa uṇṭal marapākaliṉ, aṉpuṭi marapiṉ niṉ kiḷi yeṉṟum, paliyiṭṭup paravukkaṭaṉ purivār poṟkalatti liṭṭup paṭittal vaḻakkātal paṟṟi, polampuṉi kalattiṉ eṉṟum, kākki kariyiṉ ceṉṟār mīḷuva reṉpatu vaḻakkā taliṉ, varak karintīmē eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. “veṇṇel veñcōṟu, eḻukalat tēntiṉum ciṟitueṉ tōḻi,...... viruntuvarak karinta kākkiyatu paliyē 3” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
itu naṟṟāy uṭaṉpōya talimakaḷporuṭṭuk  kākattiṟkup parāykkaṭaṉ urittatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar 1. itu nimittattoṭu paṭuttup pulampiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. 2 kāḷiyoṭu eṉṟum pāṭam.
	392.	vēyvaṉap piḻanta tōḷum veyiṟeṟa
āykaviṉ ṟolinta nutalu nōkkip
pariyal vāḻi tōḻi pariyiṉ
elliyi liṭumpi tarūum
nalvari nāṭaṉoṭu vanta māṟē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭu vanta talimakaḷ vaḻivaral varuttam kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷākiya tōḻikkuc colliyatu.
uri :
tōḻi, nalla mali cūḻnta nāṭaṉoṭu puṇarntu uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭu vantamiyāl, mūṅkiliṉatu vaṉappi iḻanta tōḷum, curattiṉkaṇ veppam varuttutalāl nuṇṇiya meṉmi aminta aḻaku kaḻinta nutalum nōkki varuntutal oḻivāyāka; varuntuviyēl, atu muṭivilla tōr tuṉpattiṉit tarumākalāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
tōḷum nutalum kaviṉ iḻattaṟku ētu veyilteṟa eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām. vēyvaṉappu, vēyoṭu nikarttaṟkuriya vaṉappu. “vēyoḻuk kaṉṉa cāyiṟip paṇittōḷ peruṅ kaviṉ 3” eṉpatu kāṇka. āykaviṉ, mikka aḻaku; āyamakaḷir palarālum ciṟappikkappeṟum aḻaku eṉṟumām; “pāvi yaṉṉa palarāy māṇkaviṉ 4” eṉṟu cāṉṟōr urippatu kāṇka. parital, varuntutal; “iruntuḷḷi eṉ parital neñcē 5” eṉṟāṟ pōla. pariyal: allīṟṟu etirmaṟi viyaṅkōḷ. aḵtu eṉac cuṭṭuvaruvikka. vantamāṟu eṉpuḻi māṟu, ētupporuḷ paṭa varuvatōr iṭiccollātaliṉ eḻuvāy ākāmi aṟintukoḷka.
taṉakkuc ciṟantāḷāy uṭaṉpayiṉṟu oḻukiṉāṭkut taṉ ṉiṭi yuḷatākum ciṟuvēṟupāṭum virintu aṟiyat tōṉṟu mākaliṉ, tōḷum nutalum, nōkkip pariyal eṉṟum, parintavaḻi ivvēṟupāṭṭiṟkut tāṉ kāraṇam eṉat tali makaṉ niṉintu periyatōr varuttam uṟuvaṉ eṉṟum, atu taṉpōlum makaḷirāl nīkkappaṭuvatu aṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvāḷ, elliyil iṭumpi tarūum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. elliyil iṭumpi tarūum eṉṟataṟkut tāyar tanti taṉṉiyar mutal palarkkum varuttam mika viḷivikkum eṉṟum, piṉṉar ataṉ viḷivu vēṟām eṉṟum karuti ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟumām. vāḻi eṉṟatu, tōḻiyiṉ aṉpuṭimikku makiḻntavāṟu.
itu mīṇṭuvanta talivi vaḻivaral varuttamkaṇṭu varun tiya tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 1
	393.	tuṟantataṟ koṇṭu tuyaraṭac cāay
aṟampulantu paḻikkum aṇka ṇāṭṭi
evva neñciṟ kēma māka
vantaṉa ḷōniṉ maṭamakaḷ
ventiṟal veḷvēl viṭalimun tuṟavē.
itu, talimakaḷ uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭu vantuḻi ayalōr avaḷtāykkuc colliyatu.
uri :
niṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟatu ētuvākak koṇṭu tuyaram varuttutalāl uṇmelintu aṟanūlkaḷi vekuṇṭu paḻikkum aṇkaṇāṭṭiyē, varuttattāl varuntum niṉ neñciṟku iṉpamuṇṭāka, vevviya valiyum veḷḷitāya vēlu muṭiya viṭaliyāvāṉ muṉvara avaṉ piṉṉē naṭantu niṉmakaḷ vantaṉaḷkāṇ  eṉṟavāṟu.
tuṟantatu, īṉṟu puṟantanta tāyākiya taṉṉit tuṟantu kātalaṉuṭaṉ koṇṭutalikkaḻintatu. cāytal, uṭampu melital. tuyaram uṇṇiṉṟu vetupputalāl uṭal melin tamiyiṉ, tuyaraṭac cāy eṉṟār. aṟam, aṟanūl; aṟaṅ kūṟuvōriyumām. aṇkaṇāṭṭi, kaṇkāṇa iruppavaḷ; “aṇka ṇāḷaṉi nakukam yāmē1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. tuyaraṅkoṇṭu varuntum neñcu evva neñcu eṉappaṭṭatu. ēmam, iṉpam; kāppumām. ō, viyappu.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukutalum koṇṭutalikkaḻitalum aṟa nūlārkku uṭaṉpāṭātaliṉ, tāy aṟatti vekuṇṭu tūṟṟiyatik kaṇṭiruntā rātaliṉ, aṟam pulantu paḻikkum aṇkaṇāṭṭi eṉṟār. aṟattip paḻittamikku ētu avaḷuṭiya makaḷ avaḷit tuṟanta tākaliṉ, tuṟantataṟ koṇṭu eṉṟum, ataṉāl āṟāt tuyar koṇṭu avalit tami tōṉṟat tuyaraṭac cāay eṉṟum ayalōr urip pārāyiṉar. evvam eṉap peyarttum kūṟiṉar, iṉi illiyā māṟu keṭutalpaṟṟi. makaḷvaravu kēṭṭu makiḻciṟantu tuyarnīṅkuva ḷākaliṉ, evva neñciṟku ēmamāka vantaṉaḷ eṉṟum, talimakaṉ maṉikkaṇ vatuvi muṭittuk koṇṭu varukiṉ ṟāḷ eṉpār, viṭali muntuṟa eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. “koṭuppōr iṉṟiyuṅ karaṇam uṇṭē, puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli  yāṉa 2” eṉpataṉāl talimakaṉ taṉ maṉikkaṇṇē koṭup pōriṉṟiyē vatuviyayartal ilakkaṇamātal aṟika. “māṟātu varupaṉi kaluḻum kaṅkuliṉ, āṉātu tuyarumem kaṇṇiṉitu paṭīiyar, emmaṉi muntuṟat tarumō, taṉmaṉi yuykkumō yātu avaṉ kuṟippē 3” eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum aṟika. aḷikaṇāṭṭi eṉṟum pāṭamuṇṭu.
	394.	māṇpil koḷkiyoṭu mayaṅkutuyar ceyta
aṉpi laṟaṉu maruḷiṉṟu maṉṟa
veñcura miṟanta vañci lōtip
perumaṭa māṉpiṇi yalitta
ciṟunutaṟ kuṟumakaṭ kāṭṭiya vammē.
iḵtu, uṭaṉpōy talimakaḷ vantuḻit tāy cuṟṟat tārkkuc colliyatu.
uri :
cuṟṟattīrē, māṇpillāta koḷkiyāl mayaṅku taṟku ētuvākiya tuyarattiṉic ceyta, aṉpoṭu kūṭāta aṟaṉum, avvaṉpiṉvaḻit tōṉṟiya aruḷi yuṭiya tāyiṟṟu. vevviya curattiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟa aḻakiya cilavākiya kūntaliyum ciṟiya nutaliyumuṭiya periya maṭappam poruntiya māṉpiṇiyi okkum iḷiyavaḷ mīṇṭu vantāḷik kāṭṭuvēṉ, vammiṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
māṇpāvatu, “koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē 1” eṉpatu. oṭu, āṉurupiṉ poruṭṭu. uṭaṉpōkkiṉi “aṟantalip piriyā āṟu 2” eṉa aṟavōr kūṟutaliṉ aṟaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. aruḷiṉṟu, aruḷ ceytatu. añcilōti, ciṟunutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ, māṉpiṇi alitta kuṟumakaḷ eṉa iyiyum. kāṭṭiya vammē eṉṟamiyiṉ, cuṟṟattīr eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. alitta eṉṟatu uvama vāyppāṭu. alittal, maruṇṭa nōkkattāl māṉpiṇiyip poruvutaliṉ, māṉiṉam taṉ iṉam eṉa maruḷuvittal. vammē, varuka eṉṉum poruṇmik kaṇ vanta muṉṉili viṉittiricol.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ uṭaṉpōkku aṟamāyiṉum, aṉpuceytu pēṇip puṟantantāri maṟantu ceyyappaṭutaliṉ, aṉpil aṟaṉum eṉṟum, īṉṟāriyum uṭaṉpayiṉ ṟoḻukiya āyattāriyum kiyikantu maṟiyiṟpuṇarntu cēṟal māṇ puṭimi aṉṟu eṉak karutukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, māṇpil koḷki yeṉṟum, akkoḷkiyāl peṟṟōrkkup pirivāṟṟāmi mēlum ayalavar kūṟum paḻippuri tōṉṟi nīṅkariya tuyarceytaliṉ, mayaṅkutuyar eṉṟum urittāḷ. ippeṟṟittāyiṉum, peṟṟōr eytiya perukiya tuyaratti māṟṟuvāṉ talimakaḷi mīḷac ceytamiyāṉ, aṉpil aṟaṉum aruḷiṉṟu maṉṟa eṉṟu icittāḷ.
itu talivi mīṇṭuvantuḻit tāy cuṟṟattārkkuk kāṭṭi yatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.1
	395.	muḷivayirp piṟanta vaḷivaḷar kūreric cuṭarviṭu neṭuṅkoṭi viṭarmuki muḻaṅkum iṉṉā varuñcuran tīrntaṉam meṉmela
ēkumati vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ pōtukalantu
kaṟaṅkici yaruvi vīḻum
piṟaṅkiruñ cōlinam perumali nāṭṭē
iḵtu uṭaṉpōy mīḷkiṉṟa talimakaṉ talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.
uri :
iḷiyavaḷē, ularnta mūṅkiliṉkaṭ piṟantu kāṟṟāl mikkup perukiya tīyiṉ oḷiviṭum neṭiya oḻuṅku, kaṟpiḷavu kaḷiṉum malimuḻiñcukaḷiṉuḷḷum muḻaṅkum koṭumi yiṉiyuṭiya cellutaṟkariya kāṭṭiṉi nīṅkiṉō mākaliṉ, iṉi pūkkaḷōṭu kūṭi olikkiṉṟa iciyiṉi yuṭiya aruvikaḷ vīḻum uyariya cōlikaḷi yuṭiya periya malikaḷ poruntiya nam nāṭṭil mella naṭantu cellu vāyāka eṉṟavāṟu.
muḷivayir, ularnta mūṅkil; “ayiriyāṟ ṟaṭikari vayiriṉ naralum kāṭu 2” eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. mūṅkil kaḷi alittut tīyip piṟappitta taṉakku ataṉi vaḷart talum kaṭaṉ eṉak karutiyatu pōla mōti mikuvittaliṉ, vaḷivaḷar kūreri eṉṟār. koṭipōṟ paṭarntu celvatu paṟṟi neṭuṅkoṭi eṉṟār;  “muḷiyariṟ piṟanta vaḷivaḷar kūreric, cuṭarnimir neṭuṅ koṭi viṭarmuki muḻaṅkum 3” eṉap piṟarum urippatu kāṇka. viṭarmuki, ummittoki. irupeyaroṭṭākkutalu moṉṟu. tīrtal, viṭutal. “tīrtalum tīrttalum viṭaṟporuṭ ṭākum 4” mati: muṉṉiliyaci. piṟaṅkutal, uyartal. iruñcōli, iruṇṭu karitāyt tōṉṟum cōli eṉṟumām.
ularnta mūṅkilkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kāṟṟāl alip puṇṭu urāyntuḻip piṟantu akkāṟṟāl mikutiyuṟṟup peruku taliṉ muḷivayirp piṟanta vaḷivaḷar kūreri eṉṟum, ēṉi maram, ceṭi, koṭikaḷiyum neṭitu toṭarntu cēṟaliṉ cuṭarviṭu neṭuṅkoṭi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. itaṉi āṇṭuk kāṭṭi viḷakkutaṟku iṭam iṉmiyiṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ. vēṉilāl viḷinta vemmiyuṭaṉ, tī vēṟu eḻuntu tuṉpam ceytaliṉ iṉṉā aruñcuram eṉṟāṉ. itukāṟum ciṟitu virintu vantami tōṉṟa, meṉmela ēkumati eṉṟāṉ, mellac celliṉ perumali nāṭṭiṉ māṇpu kaṇṭu iṉpuṟa lām eṉpatu karuttu. taṇṇeṉṟa aruviyum periya cōliyu muṭiya nāṭākaliṉ iṉṉāmi yiṉmiyāṉum, iḷiya ḷāyiṉāḷ muṟṟavum virintu celliṉ, taḷarvuṟṟu melivā ḷākalāṉum, meṉmela ēkumati eṉṟāṉ eṉṟumām. “niḻalkāṇ ṭōṟum neṭiya viki, maṇalkāṇ ṭōṟum vaṇṭal tii, varuntā tēkumati vāleyiṟ ṟōyē, mānaṉi koḻuti māṅkuyi lālum, naṟuntaṇ poḻila kāṉam, kuṟumpal lūrayām cellu māṟē 1” eṉṟu piṟāṇṭum talivaṉ kūṟutal kāṇka. nam perumali nāṭu eṉat talimakaḷi uḷappaṭut turikkiṉṟāṉ, taṉatu urimi avaṭku ivvuṭaṉ pōkkāl eytiviṭṭamiyiṉ. “urimi koṭutta kiḻavōṉ pāṅkiṉ, perumiyil tiriyā aṉpiṉ kaṇṇum 2” eṉa āciriyar kuṟippatu kāṇka.
	396.	pulippoṟi vēṅkip poṉṉiṇar koytuniṉ
katuppaya laṇiyu maḷavip pipayac
curattiṭi yayarcci yāṟuka maṭanti
kalkeḻu ciṟappi ṉammūr
elvirun tākip pukukam nāmē.
ituvumatu.
uri :
maṭantiyē, pulippoṟi pōlum vēṅki marattiṉ poṉṉiṟap pūṅkottukkaḷik koytu niṉ kūntalayal yāṉ aṇiyumaḷavum mellac ceṉṟu curattiṉkaṇ naṭan
tamiyāl eytiya ayarcciyip pōkkik koḷvāyāka; mēlum, malikaḷ poruntiya ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya nammūrkku nām pakaliṟ pōtarum viruntiṉarāyc celvōm eṉṟa
vāṟu.
vēṅkiyiṉpū pulippoṟi pōlvataṉi “ōṅkal miciya vēṅki yoḷvī, pulippoṟi kaṭuppat tōṉṟaliṉ 1” eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟuvar; ippūvi āṭavarum makaḷirum oppa aṇivar; “pūṅkaṇ vēṅkip poṉṉiṇar milintu, vāṅkami nōṉcili eruttat tirīi 2” eṉa āṭavarum, “vēṅkit taṇkamaḻ putumalar nāṟum, añcilōti 3” eṉa makaḷirum aṇivatu kāṇka. pipaya, piyappiya eṉpataṉ marūu; “pipayat taṇantaṉi yāki yuymmō 4” eṉṟami kāṇka. elviruntu, pakalē palarum kāṇac cellum viruntu. pukukam, pukuvēm.
pakaliṟ palarkāṇat talimakaḷ viruntiṉaḷāy varutaliṉ, ayarcci pōkkutalum oppaṉi ceytu kōṭalum iṉṟi yamiyāmi paṟṟi, vēṅkip poṉṉiṇar koytu aṇiyum aḷavi eṉṟum pipaya ayarcci āṟuka eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. nammūr eṉpataṟku “nam perumali nāṭu 5” eṉṟataṟku urittataṉi urittukkoḷka. elvirun tākip pukukam eṉṟatu, ayarcci āṟuka eṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam kāṭṭiya vāṟu. talimakaṉ curattik kaṭantu pōntuḻip pūṅkoytu aṇiyuṅkāl avaṉ meytīṇṭuntōṟum uṭal kuḷirp peytutalālum, pūvaṇiyumaḷavum cellātu niṟṟalālum taḷarcci nīṅkutaliṉ, ayarcci yāṟuka eṉṟāṉ.
malikeḻu nāṭu eṉṟum pāṭam.
	397.	kaviḻmayi reruttiṟ cennā yēṟṟi
kuruḷip paṉṟi koḷḷātu kaḻiyum
curaṉaṇi vārā niṉṟaṉa ḷeṉpatu
muṉṉuṟa virintanī rurimiṉ
iṉṉaki muṟuvaleṉ ṉāyat tōrkkē.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōy mīḷkiṉṟa talimakaḷ taṉṉūrkkuc cel kiṉṟārik kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu.
uri :
kīḻkkaviḻntuḷḷa mayir poruntiya kaḻuttiṉiyuṭiya cennāyiṉatu āṇ, kuṭṭiyiyuṭiya paṉṟiyik koḷ ḷātu nīṅkum curattik kaṭantu aṇittē vārāniṉṟaṉaḷ eṉa, iṉiya nakiviḷaṅkum muṟuvali yuṭiya eṉ āyat tōrkku, muṉṉāka virintu celkiṉṟa nīvir urip pīrkaḷāka eṉṟavāṟu.
eruttiṉkaṇ vaḷarntu kīḻē tāḻnta mayirik kaviḻmayir eṉṟum, aṭal mikkatu eṉṟaṟkuc cennāyēṟṟi eṉṟum kūṟiṉār, kuruḷippaṉṟi, kuruḷiyoṭu cellum paṉṟi. curattiṟkup puṟattē ūrkku aṇittāyuḷḷa nilappakuti curaṉ naṇi eṉappaṭṭatu; “ūrnaṇit tantaṉi 1” eṉṟāṟ pōla. āyattōr, āya makaḷir. “iṉṉaki āyattārōṭu iṉṉum aṟṟō 2” eṉpar piṟarum.
talivi puṇarntuṭaṉpōkiyakāli avaḷuṭiya tamar pōntu vaḷittaṉarāka, talimakaṉ taṉ kātaliyiṉ tanti taṉṉiyar eṉakkaruti aruḷuḷḷattāl vāḷātu tammoṭu koṇṭu cella viṭutta tiṟatti uḷḷuṟiyāl urittaliṉ, curaṉaṇi vārāniṉṟaṉaḷ eṉpatu eṉṟāḷ. tamar pōtarak kaṇṭa talimakaṉ, taliviyi nōkki, “nī viḷiyāṭuka ciṟitē yāṉē, maḻakaḷi ṟuriñiya parāri vēṅki, maṇaliṭu maruṅkiṉ irumpuṟam porunti, amarvariṉ añcēṉ peyarkkuveṉ, numarvariṉ maṟikuveṉ māa yōḷē 3” eṉpatum, talivi, “vāḷvaṉap puṟṟa aruvik kōḷval, eṉṉiyi maṟitta kuṉṟē aṟañcāliyarō 1” eṉpatum kāṇka. talivi mīḷaluṟum ceytiyit taṉṉiyar muṉṉuṟac ceṉṟu aṟivikkumāṟu viṭutta avar tuṇivarpāl itaṉi moḻikiṉṟā ḷākaliṉ, muṉṉuṟa virinta nīr urimiṉ eṉṟum, taṉ varavu kēṭṭu uvaki mikuntu taṉṉi etirnōkkuvar eṉpatu paṟṟi iṉ ṉaki muṟuval eṉ āyattōr eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. curaṉnaṇi vārā niṉṟaṉaḷ eṉpatu, tamar tuṇivarpāl colli viṭutta kūṟṟu. “kūṟṟavaṇ iṉmiyiṉ 2” talivi ataṉik koṇ ṭeṭuttu moḻintāḷ.
cennāyiṉ ēṟṟi kuruḷi yīṉṟa paṉṟi yeṉak karutik koḷḷātu kaḻiyum eṉṟatu, taṉ kātaliyi īṉṟa tantiyum taṉṉiyarum eṉṟu karuti ivaroṭu eṉṉic cella viṭut tāṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
